- Defect: the test increments a local `long generation` and asserts the pure static `ManagedMcpServerEntry.IsTransportGenerationCurrent` is false; `rg TransportGeneration tests` hits only this file. Deleting `++entry.TransportGeneration` or the check at `Lifecycle.cs:515` leaves it and its sibling green while the name claims restart/stale-handler coverage.
- Fix: Replace with the manager-level test of R-158 (S10-05) driving a real restart through a fake `IMcpClient`; it must go red when `++entry.TransportGeneration` is removed.
- Failing test: `McpConnectionManagerTransportEndedTests.Stale_transport_ended_handler_does_not_dispose_restarted_client` plus a mutation check removing the generation increment.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-024 · S10-07 · Infra/Mcp · `MaxJsonRpcLineBytes` and the single-allocation output cap are not enforced for external stdio or HTTP MCP servers, contrary to DESIGN
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained for HTTP; Minor for stdio) — V4: stdio servers are operator-chosen local processes; a hostile remote Streamable HTTP server is the real target; the first-party stdio reader was deliberately deleted (`McpSecurityLimitsTests.cs:287`)
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/Mcp/ConnectionManager/McpConnectionManager.Lifecycle.cs:100-123,152-153,672,695`; `...TransportFactory.cs:123-127,139-153,168-182`; `Infra/Mcp/ChannelClientTransport.cs:111,152`; `Infra/Mcp/SdkMcpClientWrapper.cs:213-242`; `Infra/Mcp/McpBridgeTool.cs:133-137,211-242`; docs `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:226,925`
- Defect: the cap is consumed only at the two in-process transport sites; stdio (`StdioClientTransport`) and Streamable HTTP read and deserialize arbitrarily long lines/bodies whole, `BoundToolDescription`/`BoundToolInputSchema` run after the entire `ListToolsResult` page is in memory, and `McpToolResultFormatter.FormatContentText` builds a full `StringBuilder` and `ToString()` before `TruncateUtf8`, so `ToolOutputCapBytes` bounds the output returned, not the allocation. No `MaxResponseContentBufferSize` exists anywhere in `src`.
- Fix: Either document the cap as in-process-only (stdio) or enforce it: for HTTP add a `SocketsHttpHandler`/stream-length bound; for stdio prefer documenting over replacing the SDK transport (the repo removed `McpStdioLineReader`). In `FormatContentText` stop appending once the UTF-8 budget is reached.
- Failing test: `McpToolResultFormatterTests.FormatContentText_stops_accumulating_at_cap` and, if enforcement is chosen, `StdioMcpServerFrameCapTests.Oversized_stdout_line_fails_the_session_instead_of_buffering`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §MCP (:226 "physical/protocol boundaries", :925 "transport frame/body cap"): state the in-process-only scope or the new external bound (see R-165)
- Design conflict: none (DESIGN implies external coverage; the sentence, not a sanctioned behavior, is wrong)

### R-025 · S11-03 · Infra/Covenant+Lexicon+Memory · Saga/Lexicon review "atomically apply" is never tested through an in-transaction late failure
- Verification: Confirmed (Saga); Narrowed for Lexicon (a mid-loop failure test exists but is vacuous) — V10
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `tests/Memory/SagaMemoryReviewServiceTests.cs:824`; `Infra/Memory/SagaMemoryReviewService.cs:404,484,528-554,560-583,621-626`; `tests/Lexicon/LexiconMemoryReviewServiceTests.cs:747`; `Infra/Lexicon/LexiconService.MemoryReview.cs:79,161-208,215-263,1391-1396`; Covenant counterpart `CovenantMemoryReviewServiceTests.cs:859`
- Defect: the only Saga "whole bulk refused" test supersedes a memory before apply, so the stale item is caught by `ValidateObservedTarget` before any `ApplyDecisionAsync` runs and nothing is ever rolled back; mutations (`continue` on a failed decision, writing receipts/marker before the loop, deleting the explicit `RollbackAsync`) stay green. The Lexicon test `One_stale_target_rolls_back_the_entire_batch` uses `Confirm`, which writes nothing, and receipts are written after the loop, so the receipt count is 0 with or without a rollback. API §8.34 says "Atomically apply or replay".
- Fix: Tests only. Saga: failure injection that survives validation, e.g. a temp trigger `BEFORE INSERT ON annal_versions WHEN NEW.Content='trigger-me' ... RAISE(ABORT)` with two Correct decisions (pick a non-busy error code because `SqliteBusyRetry` wraps the body; the exception propagates, so use `Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync` or the mapped error). Lexicon: change the existing test to `Correct` with the stale item second.
- Failing test: `SagaMemoryReviewServiceTests.A_later_decision_failure_rolls_back_earlier_corrections_receipts_and_marker` (assert first memory's content/embedding/claim head unchanged, zero receipts, marker revision unchanged) and `LexiconMemoryReviewServiceTests.One_stale_target_after_an_applied_correction_rolls_back_the_earlier_correction_receipts_and_marker`.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-026 · S31-04 · Infra/Covenant+Lexicon+Memory · Plaintext Session export is not refused while the Covenant feature flag is off, even when the Session already carries Covenant-derived artifacts
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V13: the feature-off bypass is explicitly specified and test-pinned; this is a design gap in the was-enabled-then-disabled state
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/Covenant/CovenantExportPolicy.cs:34-43` (Absent arm :38); `Api/Tower/SessionEndpoints.cs:2071,2080-2088`; `tests/Covenant/CovenantExportPolicyTests.cs:262-279`; `Infra/Data/Covenant/ICovenantConnectionSource.cs:20-35`; `Infra/Covenant/CovenantFeatureConfigurationPublisher.cs:146`
- Defect: `FeatureEnabled` is a live runtime flag but the policy reads it as "this installation has no Covenant arm" and skips `InspectSessionAsync`; `artifact_sensitivity` rows and `session_sensitivity_state` taint written while the feature was on persist after it is turned off, so Covenant-derived transcript content becomes exportable as plaintext by flipping one setting (nonrevocable egress by an already-authorized caller). DESIGN §10.19.11 :2421 says purged taint still bars export because the Session held Covenant content.
- Fix: When the feature is off, still run the two content-free ledger reads (label count + projection) over the always-present core tables via `GetOpenCoreConnectionAsync` (not the residence-latching `GetOpenConnectionAsync`), lease-less, returning `Absent` only for "tables absent or zero rows" and `PlaintextExportRefused` when any label/taint exists. Hazard: the off-arm read must not latch the one-way `CovenantProcessResidence` (it would close the offline host-tools transition on gate-off installs).
- Failing test: `CovenantExportPolicyTests.A_tainted_session_is_refused_after_the_feature_is_disabled` (`featureEnabled:false`, seed one `artifact_sensitivity` row; a second case seeding only `session_sensitivity_state` taint; assert 403 `Covenant.PlaintextExportRefused` and `repo.ExportAsync` never called; red today: 200 with transcript). Keep the existing Absent test with no ledger rows seeded.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §10.19.11 (:2419, :2437) and `docs/Arcanum.API.md` Session export section
- Design conflict: yes — `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` :2419 ("Absent means this installation has no Covenant arm and the export runs exactly as it did before the tier existed") and :2437 ("With `Arcanum:Features:Covenant` off both routes behave exactly as they did ... no lease is taken, no inventory runs") sanction the behavior; the fix changes those sentences

### R-027 · S12-01 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · Apprentice step completion is persisted on the pause/stop token after the step's tool effects have run
- Verification: Confirmed (window wider than filed) — V5: `EnableShiftingFate` defaults true, so a full model call runs on `linkedCts.Token` between stream end and `CompleteStepAsync`
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/Hosting/ApprenticeService.cs:1727,1762,1806,1815,1827-1850,2285-2362,2366-2411,2623-2645`; `Core/Configuration/ApprenticeSettings.cs:22`
- Defect: `ExecuteStepStreamAsync` has run the model and tools before the step is recorded; `CompleteStepAsync`, `FailStepAsync`/`EscalateAsync` and the Simulacrum group completion use `linkedCts.Token`, which Pause, Cancel and host shutdown cancel. A cancel between stream end and the commit (including the seconds-long Shifting Fate call) lands at the OCE arm `:1827`, which persists only `Paused`; `CurrentStep` is not advanced, resume re-runs the whole step (no idempotency, `CompletedToolCallIds` never written) and repeats file writes, commands, A2A sendings and paid inference. The same applies to the Simulacrum group commit.
- Fix: Commit step completion (status `completed`, `CurrentStep+1`) before running Shifting Fate, with `CancellationToken.None`, then run Shifting Fate and persist the plan revision separately; do the same for the group commit at `:2645` and for `FailStepAsync`/`EscalateAsync`. Hazard: `AttemptShiftingFateAsync` merges the plan tail and `CompleteStepAsync` re-reads the row, so keep plan-merge and completion coherent.
- Failing test: `ApprenticeServiceReliabilityTests.Pause_during_shifting_fate_does_not_rerun_the_completed_step` (fake provider cancels the lease token inside the Shifting Fate call; assert persisted `CurrentStep == 1`, step status `completed`, and Resume does not invoke the step prompt again) and `Pause_between_step_stream_end_and_completion_commit_still_records_the_step_completed`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §5.7 (a completed step is committed uninterruptibly)
- Design conflict: none

### R-028 · S30-04 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · A 15-second provider blip permanently drops Saga memories: the retry ladder abandons after ~15 s and the recovery gap review discards every ordinary conclusion
- Verification: Confirmed — V16 (documented design trade-off whose blast radius is unacceptable; `weave.IsAvailable` is a config-only check, the outage path is extraction-LLM failure/malformed/all-embed-fail)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/Hosting/SagaExtractionService.cs:118-122,648-690,793-840,998-1006,1048-1066,1383-1411,1444-1485`; single enqueue caller `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:7858`; test pin `tests/Hosting/SagaExtractionServiceTests.cs:2668`
- Defect: five consecutive failures (1+2+4+8 s) abandon the segment; the next turn synthesises a gap page with `HadUnprovenancedAttachmentContent: true`, `TryAuthorizeCandidate` discards every attachment-less candidate, the model page is paid for, and the cursor advances past the abandoned interval, so nothing re-reviews it. The code discards more than the prompt asks for, including conversations that never had an attachment. DESIGN :4696 and tests pin the cap, but any >15 s outage permanently loses that interval's memories.
- Fix: At abandonment while the process is alive, keep the failed segment's policy (it is in `abandoned.Segments`) and re-enqueue it on a long-ceiling schedule (the `ErasureKeyDeferralDelay` pattern at `:641`); reserve the deny-all gap policy for genuinely lost provenance (restart). Keep a ceiling (5 min) but not a count cap, or count only parse/model-shape failures toward abandonment (DESIGN :4696 "deterministic failure must not become an endless billable ladder").
- Failing test: `SagaExtractionServiceTests.ExecuteAsync_ProviderOutageBeyondLadder_StillExtractsOrdinaryMemoryOnRecovery` (fail the extraction provider 5 times with a shortened base delay, then succeed; assert the abandoned turn's attachment-free conclusion is stored; the existing `:2668` test asserts the opposite).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` Saga extraction retry/gap paragraph (~4696, 2633)
- Design conflict: partial — DESIGN :4696 ("a missing prefix is deliberately re-reviewed under deny-all provenance") and :2633 document the gap policy and cap; the fix narrows when the deny-all policy applies

### R-029 · S30-05 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · Incremental (watcher) indexing ignores the Hidden/System skip the full walk applies, so dotfile and dot-directory content is embedded and sent to the embedding provider
- Verification: Confirmed — V16 (opt-in `Features:CodebaseRetrieval`; `EyeOfTheWorldService.cs:126` shares the "hidden is never scanned" convention)
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.cs:42-52,89-215,141,171,413,431,461,566-640`; `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.Watchers.cs:317`; `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.Scheduler.cs:367`; `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceFileWatcher.cs:30-110`; `Core/Configuration/CodebaseEmbeddingSettings.cs:21-34`
- Defect: the full walk skips `FileAttributes.Hidden | System` (every dot-file and dot-directory on Unix), but a watcher `Created`/`Changed` event for `.secrets/token.json` or `.env.json` goes through `ProcessIncrementalChangesAsync`, which checks only `ContainsIgnoredDirectorySegment` and the extension (json/yaml/yml/txt/md are defaults), so the file is embedded by a possibly remote provider and stored/returned by retrieval; the next reconciliation deletes the orphan row up to 60 minutes later, after the text already reached the provider, and every edit repeats the cycle.
- Fix: One shared `IsEligible(relativePath, attributes)` used by `EnumerateCandidateFiles`, `ProcessIncrementalChangesAsync` and `QueueWatcherChange` (any dot-prefixed or Hidden/System segment, ignored segments, extension). Hazard: on Unix `File.GetAttributes` reports only the leaf's own hidden bit, so the incremental path must test every relative segment for a leading `.` itself; Windows has no dot rule, so keep the attribute check too.
- Failing test: `WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.WatcherEvents_DoNotIndexHiddenFilesTheFullWalkSkips` (write `.secrets/token.json` and `.env.json`, trigger `Created`, process pending events; assert `GetIndexedRelativePathsAsync()` empty and `weave.EmbeddedTexts` has no hidden content; red today).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` workspace indexing paragraph (~4668): state the hidden/system exclusion for both paths
- Design conflict: none

### R-030 · S30-06 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · `WatcherEvents_IgnoreExcludedFoldersAndIndexLargeFiles` does not assert that the excluded folder was ignored
- Verification: Confirmed — V16
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `tests/Weave/WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.cs:1064-1090,383-397,1248-1290`; production guard `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.cs:141-146`
- Defect: the test triggers `Created` for `node_modules/pkg/index.js` and `large.txt` but asserts only `weave.EmbedBatchCallCount > 0` and `Contains("large.txt", ...)`; deleting the `ContainsIgnoredDirectorySegment` branch indexes `node_modules/pkg/index.js` (`.js` is a default extension) and both assertions still hold. The only other ignored-segment assertion exercises the full walk, so the watcher-path guard is wholly unprotected.
- Fix: Add `Assert.DoesNotContain("node_modules/pkg/index.js".Replace('/', Path.DirectorySeparatorChar), await GetIndexedRelativePathsAsync())` and a DB-level assertion that no `workspace_file_chunks` row has a `node_modules` RelativePath (use the OS separator or it passes vacuously on Windows).
- Failing test: the same test with that assertion; confirm red by deleting `:141-146`.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-031 · S30-07 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · A failed workspace sweep retries every second forever, re-walking every registered workspace; entries registered per inference turn are never evicted
- Verification: Confirmed — V16
- Category: performance
- Location: `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.Watchers.cs:130-195` (`:189` 1 s on failure, `:150` Full on every entry); `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.Scheduler.cs:78,351-358,404,463`; `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.cs:281-285,476-480`; `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:5225`; `Api/Workspaces/WorkspaceEndpoints.cs:190,240`; test `tests/Weave/WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.Ownership.cs:159-183`
- Defect: every inference turn registers its working directory and the entry lives until process exit; when one such directory is later deleted, moved outside the allowlist, or hits an unreadable file, its unit returns `Failed`, the sweep is marked failed, the next is due in 1 s, and each retry sets `Full` on every entry, so every healthy workspace is re-walked once per second with a Warning per tick for the vanished directory. The existing test pins the 1 s retry and asserts only that the next due time is within 2 s.
- Fix: Per-entry exponential backoff (1 s doubling to the interval); retry only failed entries (set `Full` only for entries whose last outcome was Failed); evict an entry whose root identity is gone after N consecutive failures. Keep `_sweep.Outstanding` accounting for entries skipped on a retry sweep.
- Failing test: `WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.Ownership: Failed_sweep_does_not_repeat_full_walk_of_healthy_workspaces_each_second` (one healthy and one deleted workspace, ~3 s, counting file probe; healthy workspace walked at most once).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` workspace indexing scheduler paragraph (retry cadence)
- Design conflict: none

### R-032 · S30-08 · Infra/Hosting+Ops · A full tick that hits `MaxFilesToIndex` is reported Completed/Reconciled and nothing schedules the remainder
- Verification: Confirmed — V16
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Infra/Hosting/WorkspaceIndexingService.cs:329-338,413-415`; `...Scheduler.cs:339-341,380-382`; `...Watchers.cs:189-191`; `Core/Configuration/CodebaseEmbeddingSettings.cs:12-14,38-39`; test `tests/Weave/WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.cs:219-249`
- Defect: with defaults (500 files, 60 min) a 5,000-file repo gets 500 new files per Full unit; the status shows reconciled/not degraded and nothing re-queues a Full unit that stopped on the budget (the incremental path forces reconciliation), so initial indexing takes `ceil(files/500)` hours with a clean health signal. The "continues across checkpoints" test drives `IndexWorkspaceAsync` by hand, which production never does.
- Fix: When the budget stops a Full unit with changed work remaining, re-queue a Full demand after the admission yield (cap the continuation by "indexed at least one file this unit" to avoid hot-looping), or surface a `partial` bit in `WorkspaceIndexRuntimeStatus`.
- Failing test: `WorkspaceIndexingServiceTests.Scheduler: Budget_exhausted_full_tick_requeues_remaining_changed_files` (3 files, `MaxFilesToIndex=1`, one `QueueIndexNow`, wait for idle; all 3 indexed with no further manual call).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:4668` and `docs/Compendium.README.md` `MaxFilesToIndex` ("per reconciliation")
- Design conflict: none

### R-033 · S13-01 · Infra/GrimoireTransitions · Genesis mints the journal key before the anchor, so a failed anchor write leaves an unrecoverable key-only slot
- Verification: Confirmed — V3 (DESIGN :2537 sanctions the refusal for a crash/tamper reading; a failed genesis in a live process is indistinguishable)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/GrimoireTransitions/GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalStore.cs:219,287-346,878-890`; `...JournalAnchorStore.FullResetTerminal.cs:144-160`; `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:2537,2891`; test gap `JournalStoreTests.cs:166`
- Defect: `BeginAsync` persists the key credential first; if the genesis anchor `Set`/readback then fails (or the process dies, plausible with per-item macOS keychain prompts) the next `BeginAsync` sees anchor absent + files absent + key present and returns `RecoveryRequired` forever, `RecoverAsync` agrees, and the full-reset terminal proof treats it as residue. Nothing clears it, so every Covenant reset and factory erasure on the profile is disabled until the operator hand-deletes a keychain item, though no transition was ever sealed (epoch 0).
- Fix: Make genesis crash-consistent: write the closed epoch-0 anchor first, then mint the key; let `BeginAsync` allow `existing is { SlotEpoch: 0, State: Closed }` with key `NotFound` to `CreateOrOpen`. Keep the key-without-anchor refusal for the post-transition/tamper case and keep the terminal arm treating "anchor present, key absent" as not terminal; update the DESIGN residue wording in the same change.
- Failing test: `GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalStoreTests.Begin_recovers_after_genesis_anchor_write_fails_once` (fail `anchor:genesis-written` via `failBeforeStep` on the first `BeginAsync`, healthy second `BeginAsync` succeeds; today `RecoveryRequired`).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:2537,:2891` (key/anchor ordering and residue wording)
- Design conflict: partial — DESIGN :2537 calls a key with no anchor "the residue of a genesis that began"; the fix keeps the tamper guard and relaxes only the closed epoch-0 case

### R-034 · S13-02 · Infra/GrimoireTransitions · Windows "exchange" is two non-atomic renames; a failure or crash between them strands the journal with no canonical file
- Verification: Confirmed — V3 (Windows-only, shipping RIDs; rev N stays intact in `previous` so a recovery arm can restore it)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/GrimoireTransitions/GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalFilePrimitives.cs:545-621,597-611,1568-1625`; `...JournalFileStore.cs:618-640,2008-2026`; `...JournalStore.cs:764,925-947`
- Defect: macOS/Linux use a single atomic kernel swap (`renameatx_np`/`renameat2`); the Windows arm renames journal→previous then working→journal via `NtSetInformationFile` (class 10, ReplaceIfExists FALSE). If rename 2 fails (sharing violation, AV lock), `ReplaceDurablyAsync` treats it as pre-publication and unlinks the fully written working file; a death between the renames leaves `previous`+`working` and no canonical. `RecoverAsync` has no arm for `Canonical == null` with an Active anchor above revision 0 and returns `RecoveryRequired`, so the transition guarding destructive Covenant effects is unresumable.
- Fix: On rename-2 failure roll back `previous -> journal` with `MoveNoReplace` before returning, and add a `Canonical == null && Previous != null` recovery arm (authenticate `previous` as anchor-current, optionally `working` as one-ahead; restore `previous` to canonical, then run the existing one-ahead flow). The rollback cannot cover a crash between the renames, so the recovery arm is required. Do not use `File.Replace`/`ReplaceFileW` (re-introduces the reparse-point hazard the code comment avoids).
- Failing test: `GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalFileStoreTests.Windows_exchange_second_rename_failure_restores_canonical` (via the `IGrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalFilePrimitives` fake failing the second rename) and `GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalStoreTests.Recover_restores_canonical_from_previous_when_canonical_absent`; both red today.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:2537` (crash-convergence claim; add the platform note)
- Design conflict: none

### R-035 · S14-01 · Infra/Repos+Config+DI · The `CommLinkWebhook` named client's egress-guard handler (redirect refusal, DNS-rebind pinning) has no test
- Verification: Confirmed — V4 (exploitation needs a regression; stands as a test-honesty gap on a documented SSRF guarantee)

- Location: `Infra/DependencyInjection/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:1568-1583`; `Infra/CommLink/WebhookCommLinkDispatcher.cs:19,76-90,101`; `tests/CommLink/WebhookCommLinkDispatcherTests.cs:20-56`; `tests/Logging/NamedHttpClientLoggingTests.cs:20-60`; precedent `tests/Api/ApiSurfaceContractTests.cs:123-147`

Removing `.ConfigurePrimaryHttpMessageHandler(static () => OutboundUrlGuard.CreateUntrustedEgressHandler())` leaves all green, yet a webhook host could 302 to `169.254.169.254` or rebind DNS.
- Fix: Add a composition test mirroring `ProviderInferenceHttpClient_uses_the_pinned_provider_egress_handler` for `"CommLinkWebhook"` and `ArcanumBrowseWebConstants.HttpClientName`.

### R-036 · S14-02 · Infra/Repos+Config+DI · The Familiar status probe's secret-environment denylist is never exercised

The `settings is null → []` default also fails open (production does supply settings).

### R-037 · S15-01 / V-S15-01 · Infra/Weave+Intelligence · Session-scope Tapestry trees are keyed uppercase but looked up lowercase, so they are never retrieved or counted
- Verification: Confirmed (Blocking → Important) — V7: opt-in (`Features.Tapestry` defaults false, `PublicConfigurationSettings.cs:46`); `GetScopeStatusesAsync`/`CountPublishedNodesAsync` have no production caller, but the wired reader at `MemoryEndpoints.cs` has the same defect (V-S15-01, Confirmed)
- Category: correctness
- Location: write key `Infra/Weave/TapestryStore.cs:36-40` (ids copied from `Entries.SessionId`, uppercase by `Infra/Data/Schema/Triggers/Entries_SessionId_guard_identity_insert.sql`/`_update.sql`); `Infra/Hosting/TapestryWeavingService.cs:124-127`; read key `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:5513,5517,5536-5541` (lowercase `sessionId.ToString()`), `Infra/Weave/TapestryStore.cs:289-312` (`"ScopeId" = @scopeId`, BINARY), `:1075,:1115`; second reader `Api/Tower/MemoryEndpoints.cs:941-945` (`g.ScopeId = @sessionId`, bound at `:1548-1560`; `@canonicalSessionId` is bound but unused here); `Infra/Repositories/SessionEntryPersistence.cs:961`
- Defect: `Entries.SessionId` is guaranteed uppercase-dashed, so sweeps store Session-kind generations under uppercase `ScopeId`; retrieval builds `TapestryScope(Session, sessionId.ToString())` (lowercase) and `tapestry_generations.ScopeId` has no NOCASE collation, so `GetCurrentGenerationAsync` returns null and the session tree is silently skipped (`continue`), while every sweep pays summary and embedding spend for trees no turn reads. The `SessionAttachment` kind works only because `session_attachment_chunks.SessionId` is deliberately lowercase. The status endpoint's per-session count compares one parameter against both spellings and cannot match both kinds, so it stays wrong after the retrieval fix.
- Fix: Normalise per kind at the READ side, not in the store (V7 hazard: uppercasing both kinds in `TapestryStore` orphans every attachment-scoped generation). At `WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:5517` use `sessionId.ToString("D").ToUpperInvariant()` for the Session kind only, leave `:5513` (SessionAttachment) lowercase, put the per-kind spelling in one helper (`TapestryScope.ForSession(Guid)`/`ForSessionAttachment(Guid)`), and do not touch stored ids. Fix `MemoryEndpoints.cs:941-945` together: `(g.ScopeKind='Session' AND g.ScopeId=@canonicalSessionId) OR (g.ScopeKind='SessionAttachment' AND g.ScopeId=@sessionId)`. Sequence with R-224 (S15-09): fixing this activates the Entry-purge exposure.
- Failing test: `TapestryStoreTests.Session_scope_generation_is_found_by_the_scope_the_retrieval_path_builds` (seed an `Entries` row via the production writer, `DiscoverScopesAsync` + weave, build the scope with the helper `WizardIntelligenceProvider` uses, assert `GetCurrentGenerationAsync(...)` non-null; better a `TapestryRetrievalTests` case through the provider) and a `MemoryEndpointsTests` count test seeding a Complete Session-kind (uppercase) and SessionAttachment-kind (lowercase) generation for one session and asserting both nodes counted. No test exercises `TapestryScopeKind.Session` end to end today.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §21.11 (state the per-kind scope-id spelling)
- Design conflict: none

### R-038 · S15-02 · Infra/Weave+Intelligence · Tapestry summarizer feeds untrusted corpus text to a model that still has native tools (web read/search, delegate_task)
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V7: exfiltration channel exists only if `Features.WebBrowsing` (default false) and `Features.Tapestry` (default false) are on; the `delegate_task` leg was not verified (`SubagentRunner.cs` line 261 `DisableAllTools` claim not reproduced in the verifier's grep)
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/Weave/TapestrySummarizer.cs:19,138-144,151`; `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:4428,6421-6522` (`BuildToolSetWithMcpAsync`), `:6520-6522` (`ShouldDisableMcpTools` guards only the MCP block); `Infra/Weave/TapestryWeaver.cs:893`
- Defect: the summarizer sets `SkipSpellRouting`, `DisableMcpTools`, `UnattendedMode` but not `DisableAllTools`/`ToolPolicy`; native tools (`get_local_time`, `get_system_info`, `delegate_task`, spell-script when roots exist, `web_search`/`read_url` when web browsing is enabled) are added before the MCP short-circuit and `FilterToolsForUnattended` removes only `ask_human`. The excerpts are arbitrary workspace files, attachments and transcripts, so a hostile file can ask the model to call `read_url` with the corpus in the query string, on an unattended timer. The Saga and Logger paths share the gap.
- Fix: Add `DisableAllTools: true` (or `ToolPolicy: NoTools`, the more consistent route since the request-level policy drives the filter) to the `PingRequest` in `SummarizeAsync`; consider the same for `SagaExtractionService.cs:1136` and `Loremaster.cs:344`. Check `DisableAllTools` does not suppress system-prompt sections the summarizer relies on.
- Failing test: `TapestrySummarizerTests.SummarizeAsync_requests_no_tools` (fake `IArcanumIntelligenceProvider` captures the `PingRequest`; assert `DisableAllTools` true or `ToolPolicy == NoTools`; red today).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §21.11 and §22.2 (headless-call tool posture)
- Design conflict: none

### R-039 · S15-04 · Infra/Weave+Intelligence · A failed or cancelled Tapestry build discards every summary it paid for, and the failure retries each sweep
- Verification: Confirmed — V7 (opt-in feature; no negative cache, backoff or resume found)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/Weave/TapestryStore.cs:529-563,799-840` (`TryGetReusableSummaryAsync` reads only `g."Status" = 'Complete'` at :816-817; `AbandonGenerationAsync`); `Infra/Weave/TapestryWeaver.cs:160-182,273,327,870-910` (embed failure at :906 after the paid call); `Infra/Hosting/TapestryWeavingService.cs:51-60,127-160`
- Defect: summaries are persisted into the `Building` generation but reuse looks only in `Complete` generations and abandonment deletes the Building rows; if cluster k of n fails (provider hiccup, budget refusal, a summary the embedder rejects) the first k-1 paid summaries are discarded, the corpus fingerprint is unchanged so `IsUpToDate` is false, and the next tick (default 60 min) repeats the whole paid prefix; a deterministic failure re-bills every earlier summary forever.
- Fix: Simplest bounded fix: a per-(scope, corpus fingerprint) failure counter with exponential backoff in `TapestryWeavingService`. Resumable Building generations are larger and interact with `AbandonGenerationAsync` and reconciliation, so do not delete Building rows while a resume is allowed.
- Failing test: `TapestryWeaverTests.WeaveAsync_ASummaryFailureDoesNotRebillEarlierClustersOnTheNextAttempt` (count `SummarizeAsync` calls across two weaves of an unchanged corpus; fail the 3rd summary once; today re-billed).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §21.11 (failure/retry economics)
- Design conflict: none

### R-040 · S15-05 · Infra/Weave+Intelligence · The real `TapestrySummarizer` has no test at all
- Verification: Confirmed — V7
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `Infra/Weave/TapestrySummarizer.cs:50-273` (`ComputeFenceLength` at :239); tests reference only fakes: `tests/Weave/Tapestry/TapestryWeaverTests.cs:914`, `TapestryAdmissionHarness.cs:289-295`
- Defect: the model fallback chain (`SummaryModel` → `FastModel` → `DefaultModel`), the `FitsOneRequest` arithmetic (`2L *` reservation), the UNTRUSTED framing and adaptive backtick fence, the tool/spell flags and the failure mapping are unexercised; no test constructs the class. Every listed mutation (fixed 3-backtick fence, `1L *`, dropped `UnattendedMode`, reversed fallback) stays green, yet this is the only component sending corpus text to a model.
- Fix: Add a `TapestrySummarizerTests` class with a fake `IArcanumIntelligenceProvider`/`IModelTokenEstimator`.
- Failing test: `TapestrySummarizerTests.BuildUserPrompt_uses_a_fence_longer_than_any_backtick_run_in_the_excerpt`, `ResolveSummaryModel_prefers_SummaryModel_then_FastModel_then_DefaultModel`, `FitsOneRequest_reserves_twice_MaxSummaryTokens`.

- Design conflict: none

### R-041 · S15-08 · Infra/Weave+Intelligence · Spell export bypasses the scanner's hardened read path (symlink and FIFO)
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V7: operator-initiated and local; the SPELL.md read is mostly shielded because `GetAsync` already loaded it through the scanner; open gap is `scripts/*` and the sidecar; `ActivateVersionAsync` (:1018) has the same read pattern
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/Intelligence/Spells/SpellRepository.cs:455-566` (sidecar `File.ReadAllTextAsync` ~:496 behind `TryGetFileLength` :486, SPELL.md ~:515, scripts `Directory.EnumerateFiles` :524 + `File.ReadAllBytesAsync` ~:548), `:1018,:1570` (`TryGetFileLength` follows symlinks); contrast `Infra/Workspaces/SpellScanner.cs:1284-1301,1345-1366`; `Api/Tower/SpellAuthoringEndpoints.cs:100-125`
- Defect: a symlinked `scripts/loot -> ~/.ssh/id_ed25519` in a cloned spell directory is returned base64 in the export envelope (the per-file cap does not help because `FileInfo.Length` follows the link); a FIFO stats as length 0 and blocks the open, wedging a request thread. The scanner's own comment explains the hazard; export still uses the weak pattern.
- Fix: Reuse `SecureFileReader` + `TryGetRegularFileLength` + `WorkspacePathPolicy.RevalidatePathBeforeIo`, skipping non-regular files. Hazard: scripts are binary, so a byte-returning secure read is needed (the scanner's helper is UTF-8 text); do not switch to text and re-encode.
- Failing test: `SpellRepositoryTests.ExportAsync_skips_a_script_symlinked_outside_the_spell_directory` (symlink to a secret file; export has no entry and the secret bytes are absent) and `ExportAsync_skips_a_fifo_script` (POSIX-only, with a timeout); neither exists.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md` spell export section; `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §11.6
- Design conflict: none

### R-042 · S16-03 · Infra/Process+A2A · Windows AppContainer ACL restore is snapshot-based, so overlapping runs corrupt each other's grants
- Verification: Plausible — V7 (needs overlapping sandboxed runs sharing a root; Windows-only; effect is spurious access-denied mid-run and dead-SID ACE residue, not escalation)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/Process/WindowsAppContainerLauncher.cs:88-137,143-173`; `Infra/Process/WindowsAppContainerRestoreJournal.cs:120-169`; `Infra/Process/CappedChildProcessRunner.cs:937`; `Infra/Process/ChildProcessFilesystemJail.cs:184-224`
- Defect: each broker captures the root's whole DACL, adds its own ACE and later restores the whole captured DACL; run A restoring D0 deletes B's live ACE while B runs, and B restoring D0+ACE(A) permanently re-adds the dead SID A, the orphaned-ACE accumulation the journal exists to prevent. Nothing serialises runs per root.
- Fix: Restore by removing this run's SID ACEs from a freshly read DACL (journal the SID, not the descriptor) or hold a per-root cross-process lock for the grant..restore span. The journal `Replay` (host-side) must change together because it replays whole descriptors.
- Failing test: Windows-only `WindowsAppContainerAclTests.Overlapping_grants_restore_without_removing_the_other_runs_ace` (two `Grant` calls on one temp dir; restore the first, assert the second SID's ACE survives; restore the second, assert the DACL equals the original).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` ~3503 ("Original security descriptors are restored in reverse order")
- Design conflict: none

### R-043 · S16-04 · Infra/Process+A2A · No test exercises the Windows broker or `ApplyWindows` success path, yet docs and source comments claim Windows CI covers it
- Verification: Confirmed — V7 (hides R-004 and R-005)
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `Infra/Process/WindowsAppContainerLauncher.cs:20` (`[ExcludeFromCodeCoverage] // ... exercised by Windows CI`); `Infra/Process/ChildProcessFilesystemJail.cs:226-317`; `tests/Process/ChildProcessFilesystemJailTests.cs:288-337,340-436,1003`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml:441,591`; `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:3826,3998`
- Defect: the Windows tests assert only fail-closed behaviour when `SandboxExecHelper.IsBrokerCapableHost` is false (it is false in the xunit host by design) and the journal on synthetic descriptors; `LaunchSuspended`, `Grant`, `CreateProcessW`, profile creation, `WaitUntilAssignedToJob` and real restore never run anywhere, and no script/workflow runs `execute_command` or `__sandbox-exec`. Replacing `CreateProcessW(payload.Target, ...)` with `return 0` turns nothing red.
- Fix: Add a Windows-lane smoke that runs the published apphost with a real broker (`cmd.exe /c echo ok` through `CappedChildProcessRunner` with `ForExecuteCommand` roots and default limits) and a real-DACL round trip for `RestoreDirectorySecurity`; correct the comment and DESIGN lines until it exists.
- Failing test: `WindowsAppContainerBrokerTests.Broker_runs_a_bare_command_inside_the_jail_and_restores_the_workspace_dacl` (Skip.IfNot Windows; assert exit 0, stdout, workspace DACL byte-equal before/after, no leftover profile); must run against the published `arcanum.exe` or a host registering `SandboxExecHelper.TryHandle`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:3826` and `:3998` (correct the claim or add the test)
- Design conflict: none

### R-044 · S16-06 · Infra/Process+A2A · Callback-mode registration is awaited with the caller's token outside the `try`, so cancellation orphans the remote task and its ledger lease
- Verification: Confirmed — V7 (recoverable on restart through `A2AOutboundSendingRecoveryHandler`; "until the host restarts", not forever)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/A2A/A2AClientService.cs:589-597,604-619,773-800` (catch filter :791); `Infra/A2A/A2ASendingLedger.cs:990`; test `tests/A2A/A2APushNotificationTests.cs:464-500`
- Defect: the remote task already exists and the ledger entry is Tracked (renewer holding the lease) when `TryRegisterCallbackAsync(…, cancellationToken)` runs outside the `try` that starts at :604; its catch handles only `HttpRequestException or A2AException`, so a caller cancel during the registration RPC throws `OperationCanceledException` straight out of `ExchangeAsync`, skipping the peer `tasks/cancel` + `ReleaseLedgerAsync` path. The peer keeps running and billing and `A2ASendingLeaseRenewer` renews the row every 5 minutes for the host's lifetime.
- Fix: Move `TryRegisterCallbackAsync` inside the existing `try` (or wrap it so an OCE runs the same `TryCancelRemoteTaskAsync` + `ReleaseLedgerAsync`); keep the cancel/release on their own bounded token and make `callback?.Dispose()` in the `finally` tolerate a null `callback`.
- Failing test: `A2APushNotificationTests.DispatchSendingAsync_CallbackModeCancelledDuringRegistration_StillCancelsTheRemoteTaskAndReleasesTheLedger` (handler blocks the `CreateTaskPushNotificationConfig` request until cancel; assert a `tasks/cancel` arrives and the ledger entry is closed); the existing test cancels only after the request is observed.
- Docs: none (DESIGN §5.7.1 already documents cancellation propagating to the peer)
- Design conflict: none

### R-045 · S16-07 · Infra/Process+A2A · Any non-`HttpRequestException/A2AException` after the remote task is accepted leaves the ledger lease renewing forever
- Verification: Plausible — V7 (depends on which exceptions the A2A SDK surfaces; `response.Task ?? throw InvalidOperationException` at :567 is in our own code; the subscribe path already catches `IOException`/`InvalidOperationException`)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Infra/A2A/A2AClientService.cs:337,547,567,604-637,1170-1177,1206-1234,1224-1232`
- Defect: the post-registration `try` has handlers only for `OperationCanceledException` and `HttpRequestException or A2AException`; a malformed peer body (JSON error, null `Status`, IO error on the poll path) escapes with no `StopRenewing` and no peer cancel, so the Tracked ledger row is renewed for the host's lifetime and reconciliation never cancels the still-running remote task or releases the budget reservation; `ConnectAsync` (:337) has the same narrow filter and turns a malformed card into an unhandled exception from the tool call.
- Fix: Add a final `catch (Exception ex) when (ex is not OperationCanceledException)` that calls `StopRenewing(ledgerEntry)` (leaving the row open for reconciliation like the transport-loss branch) and returns an `AgentUnreachable`/`AgentCardInvalid` failure; widen the `ConnectAsync` filter to `JsonException`/`FormatException`.
- Failing test: `A2ASendingOutcomeTests.DispatchSendingAsync_PeerReturnsMalformedTaskWhilePolling_StopsRenewingAndReturnsFailure` (handler returns invalid JSON on `tasks/get`; assert a failure `Result` and `A2ASendingLeaseRenewer` no longer holds the entry); runs on macOS, so one run decides Plausible vs Confirmed.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-046 · S16-08 · Infra/Process+A2A · Outbound A2A client has no response-size cap and no idle-I/O deadline (card fetch, JSON-RPC, SSE) on an untrusted-host path
- Verification: Plausible (repository half confirmed) — V7 (SDK card-resolver internals not read; cancellation by turn/host ends a stall, so it is slot-exhaustion for unattended Apprentices)
- Category: security
- Location: `Infra/DependencyInjection/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:1510-1518`; `Infra/A2A/A2AClientService.cs:269-272,1398-1445,1138-1160,545`; `Infra/A2A/A2APushNotifications.cs:270-285`; `Infra/Security/OutboundUrlGuard.cs:296-321`; test comment `tests/A2A/A2AOutboundHttpClientTests.cs:16`
- Defect: the named client has `Timeout = InfiniteTimeSpan`, the handler sets only `AllowAutoRedirect=false`, `UseProxy=false` and a connect callback; no `MaxResponseContentBufferSize` (default 2 GiB) or idle read timeout exists, and the global A2A slot (`MaxConcurrentA2ATasks`) is taken before discovery. A hostile peer named by a prompt-injected Apprentice (`agent_url` is model-supplied; default allowlist empty) can stream an unbounded card/RPC body or stall the socket pinning a slot; the push post also buffers the full response within its 15 s window. The repo already solved this for web research (`LocalHttpWebProvider.cs:265-295`).
- Fix: A bounded linked deadline (e.g. 30 s) for discovery only (not the Sending, which per issue #55 has none), `HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead` with a capped stream read for the card and push post, and `MaxResponseContentBufferSize` on the named client. Do not apply the discovery deadline to the SSE subscription or poll loop.
- Failing test: `A2AClientTests.DispatchSendingAsync_PeerStallsCardResponse_FailsWithinDiscoveryDeadlineAndReleasesTheSlot` (handler never completes the card response; assert failure and that a second dispatch can acquire the slot) and `..._OversizedCard_IsRejected`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §5.7.1 (state the discovery deadline and body cap); fix the comment at `tests/A2A/A2AOutboundHttpClientTests.cs:16`
- Design conflict: none

### R-047 · S03-04 / S29-17 · Infra/Security · `DataProtectionSecretStore` reports any non-NotFound read failure as "corrupt" and steers the operator to delete security.dat and the Grimoire
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V2: the destructive "remove both security.dat and the Grimoire .db" text is only on the API-key store; no automated path acts destructively on the status (the bootstrapper fails closed with a Grimoire present); harm is misleading guidance
- Category: usability
- Location: `Infra/Security/DataProtectionSecretStore.cs:21-24,51,98-102,120-127,147-200` (non-Success/non-NotFound → `Corrupted` at :170-173), `:163-171`; `Infra/Security/SecureFileReader.cs:19-28`; consumers `OsKeychainSecretStore.cs:112,349`, `ArcanumMasterKeyBootstrapper.cs:118-141`, `Cli/Commands/KeyCommands.cs:77`, `DoctorCommand.cs:880`, `ArcanumApiCredentialFailureMapper.cs:47`
- Defect: `SecureFileReader.ReadBytesAsync` can fail transiently or for non-corruption reasons (`IoError`, `AccessDenied`, `TooLarge`, `Rejected` for a hard link/symlink), yet all fold into `SecretStoreReadResult.Corrupted("... could not be decrypted (corrupt or wrong Data Protection key ring)...")`; only a `CryptographicException` is a decryption failure. A transient EACCES/EIO on a store guarding an encrypted DB therefore yields operator guidance that can destroy the Grimoire.
- Fix: Add a distinct `Unreadable` status (or message) for `AccessDenied`/`IoError`/`TooLarge`/`Rejected` with retry/permissions text; reserve the destructive recovery text for `CryptographicException` and a zero-length file. Hazard: `ArcanumMasterKeyBootstrapper` and `OsKeychainSecretStore` branch on `Corrupted`, so `Unreadable` must also fail closed there (extend `ThrowIfCorruptedWithExistingGrimoire` and the probe path; never regenerate).
- Failing test: `DataProtectionSecretStoreTests.ReadProtected_AccessDenied_DoesNotReportCorrupted_OrDestructiveGuidance` / `An_unreadable_file_is_not_reported_as_corrupt` (chmod 000 as a Unix `[SkippableFact]` or injected `SecureFileReader` failure; assert status is not `Corrupted` and the message lacks "remove both").
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Engineering.md` "Local Grimoire reinstall"; credential-status table in `docs/Arcanum.API.md` if `Corrupted` is documented
- Design conflict: none

### R-048 · S29-01 · Infra/Security · Request-path API-key auth does a blocking, un-timed, un-cancellable, un-coalesced Keychain read every 30 s and on every request while it fails (and the same unguarded read exists at startup)
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V17: only the gate holder blocks a thread (waiters hold none), so it is an auth wedge not thread starvation; the hang needs a Keychain fault mid-process; the known post-rebuild prompt is most directly the STARTUP read at `ArcanumMasterKeyBootstrapper.cs:96`, which the finder did not name
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Security/ApiKeyAuthenticator.cs:61,171-193` (finder's :179 is :181); `Infra/Security/OsKeychainSecretStore.cs:174-182` (`_gate.WaitAsync()` no token, then synchronous `_osStore.TryGet`), `:57-61,306-309,375-378`; `Infra/Security/ProviderCredentialStore.cs:89-94,181-186`; `Infra/Security/WebResearchCredentialStore.cs:49-53,111-115`; startup `Infra/Security/ArcanumMasterKeyBootstrapper.cs:96`; `Secrets/Security/MacOsCredentialStore.cs` (plain P/Invoke, no timeout/Task guard); `Core/Configuration/ArcanumRuntimeDefaults.cs:19` (TTL 30 s)
- Defect: on every digest-cache miss the authenticator awaits `OsKeychainSecretStore._gate` (no timeout, no token) and calls the Keychain synchronously; a Keychain dialog nobody dismisses parks the holder and queues every header-bearing request behind one stuck call with no way to abort. There is no single-flight (N concurrent misses do N serial reads) and a failed read caches nothing, so each header-bearing request, including bogus-key ones, re-enters the Keychain during a fault. `CampaignRootIdentityKeyProvider` and `MemoryErasureKeyring` already solve this by latching.
- Fix: Wrap the OS `TryGet` in a bounded call (`Task.Run(...).WaitAsync(timeout, ct)`, accounting for the abandoned blocked thread) while keeping `_gate` held until the call truly returns (releasing on timeout stacks prompts); use a "read in flight, last result" latch like `MemoryErasureKeyring` so a timed-out/failed read fails closed immediately for N seconds; re-check the digest cache after taking the gate; cache only a short negative outcome, never extend a stale positive; apply the same guard to the startup read at `ArcanumMasterKeyBootstrapper.cs:96`/`GetApiKeyReadResultAsync`; plumb the request token through `PeekApiKeyReadResultAsync`.
- Failing test: `OsKeychainSecretStoreTests.Peek_does_not_hold_the_gate_past_a_read_timeout` (`IOsCredentialStore.TryGet` blocks on a `ManualResetEventSlim`; a second `PeekApiKeyReadResultAsync` completes with `Corrupted` within the timeout); `ApiKeyAuthenticatorTests.Concurrent_misses_issue_one_store_read` (1 read for 20 concurrent requests) and `A_failed_read_is_not_retried_per_request`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §11.2 (API-key storage / digest cache) and §4.3 auth description
- Design conflict: none

### R-049 · S29-04 · Infra/Security · No known-answer vector pins the Grimoire KDF, so any constant change silently makes existing databases unopenable
- Verification: Confirmed — V17
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `Infra/Security/GrimoireKeyDerivation.cs:11-19,53-75,77-111`; `tests/Security/GrimoireKeyDerivationTests.cs:50-66,68-83,118-130,145-157`; circular legacy-upgrade tests `tests/Hosting/GrimoireDatabaseBootstrapperTests.cs:215-248,250-283`; `tests/Fixtures/GrimoireFixture.cs:112`
- Defect: the persisted-format KDF (PBKDF2-SHA256, 600,000 iterations, 32-byte output; legacy HKDF salt/info strings) has no literal expected passphrase anywhere: assertions are determinism, length 44, inequality and argument validation, and every fixture derives through the production function. `IterationCount = 1`, `SHA256 → SHA1`, editing `LegacySalt`/`LegacyInfo`/`DedicatedInfo`, or a BOM/encoding change all keep every test green while making existing user databases unopenable (data-loss class).
- Fix: Add hard-coded vectors computed once by an independent implementation (`openssl kdf` or Python `hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac`) for PBKDF2 (fixed secret, fixed 16-byte salt, 600,000 iterations) and both legacy HKDF variants (`DerivePassphraseFromApiKeyLegacy`, `DerivePassphraseFromEncryptionSecretLegacy`); add `Assert.Equal(600_000, IterationCount)` (the vector is what pins SHA-256 and the encoding) and keep the length-44 assertion.
- Failing test: `GrimoireKeyDerivationTests.Pbkdf2_matches_the_published_vector` and `Legacy_hkdf_variants_match_published_vectors` (red against any constant mutation).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §5.4 (state the KDF as a frozen contract pinned by the vector test)
- Design conflict: none

### R-050 · S17-02 · Api/Wizard · Buffered turn that fails to begin its Grimoire turn returns generic `Hub.Error`, dropping `Hub.SessionTurnBusy` (409) and every other typed begin failure
- Verification: Confirmed — V18
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:1706-1725` (Error frame at :1722, `BufferedTerminal` never set), `:948-960` (drain falls back to `Hub.Error`), `:251-255` (buffered emitter drops Error frames); sibling branches that do set it `:1662,1787,2061,2134`; `Api/Intelligence/GrimoireTurnWriter.cs:824-829`; `Api/Intelligence/IntelligenceEndpoints.cs:93`
- Defect: `BeginAssistantReplyCoreAsync` returns typed failures (`Hub.SessionTurnBusy`, `Session.CampaignBindingRequired`, `Grimoire.WriteFailed`, entry-limit errors) but the buffered begin branch only yields an Error event, so the caller gets `Hub.Error` / `PublicInferenceFailureMessage` (500-class) instead of the 409 documented at `docs/Arcanum.API.md:720` and `ArcanumErrorMapper.cs:156`; a client racing two turns on one Session cannot retry correctly.
- Fix: Set `classification.BufferedTerminal = Result<PromptTurnResult>.Failure(begun.Error)` before the `yield` at `:1722`, mirroring the other branches (the streaming begin sites `:1749`/`:2357` belong to R-054). Retry keying on `IsConnectivityFailure` stays false, so no behavior change there.
- Failing test: `WizardIntelligenceProviderTests.ExecutePromptAsync_SessionTurnBusy_ReturnsTypedConflict` (hold the gate with a first turn on session S, call `ExecutePromptAsync` with `SessionId = S`, assert `result.Error.Code == ErrorCodes.Hub.SessionTurnBusy`; the existing assertion at `:1280` calls the writer directly).
- Docs: none (`docs/Arcanum.API.md:720` already states the contract)
- Design conflict: none

### R-051 · S17-03 · Api/Wizard · Provider fallback after a connectivity failure sends the user prompt twice to the next candidate on session-bound requests
- Verification: Confirmed — V18
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:735,1148,1541,1680-1682,1700-1705,2088-2092`; `Api/Intelligence/InferenceContextBuilder.cs:108-122,275,333-378`; `Infra/Repositories/GrimoireRepository.SessionTurnBegin.cs:94-190`; `Infra/Repositories/GrimoireRepository.cs:551-590`
- Defect: candidate 1 begins the turn (inserting the user Entry and an empty assistant row) and is deferred without discarding it; candidate 2 reloads the Session thread, which now contains that user Entry, `MapGrimoireToMeAiMessages` strips only the empty assistant row and appends `newUserPrompt` again, so the fallback provider sees two consecutive identical user messages (extra tokens billed, a doubled instruction) on every session-bound fallback. Candidate 1 loaded before the begin, and session-less requests are unaffected.
- Fix: Capture the thread once in the first attempt into `TurnContextSeed` (before any begin) and reuse it for later candidates, or when `seed.Turn` is adopted drop the trailing user Entry equal to the begun turn's user entry id. Reuse the same snapshot in the compression/watermark logic (`InferenceContextBuilder.cs:275`).
- Failing test: `WizardIntelligenceProviderFallbackTests.ExecutePromptAsync_fallback_sends_prompt_once_to_second_candidate_for_existing_session` (session-bound request; fake grimoire whose `GetSessionAsync` includes the entries `BeginCalls` appended; candidate A throws `HttpRequestException`, B records messages; assert exactly one user message with the prompt).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §10.7.2 (the thread snapshot is part of the seed)
- Design conflict: none

### R-052 · S17-04 · Api/Wizard · Streaming provider timeout (non-caller `OperationCanceledException`) escapes classification, so the seeded turn is finalized and the fallback candidate runs on a dead turn handle
- Verification: Plausible — V18 (could not prove the shipped provider stack surfaces a timeout as `TaskCanceledException` from the stream enumeration; HttpClient.Timeout does not apply after headers, but connect/response-header timeouts are likely)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:3128-3194` (unconditional OCE catch :3153-3178; generic catch sets `IsConnectivityFailure` :3180-3194), `:4325-4350` (finally), `:1245-1296,1323-1374,1700-1705,8044-8088`; `Api/Intelligence/ModelCallExecutor.cs:143,226-235` (streaming has no catch, buffered filters on the caller token); `Api/Intelligence/GrimoireTurnWriter.cs:213-221,268-273,1040-1100`
- Defect: if the provider stream surfaces a timeout as `TaskCanceledException`/OCE with a live caller token, the iterator rethrows through its `finally` with a default classification so `ShouldDeferTurnToNextCandidate` is false, the empty assistant row is discarded, the handle marked `IsFinalized` and the Session lease released; the wrapper then classifies the exception as connectivity and starts candidate 2 on the same finalized handle, whose finalize does not check `IsFinalized`, so the fallback answer is lost (or persisted against a discarded row) without an error, in exactly the scenario fallback exists for.
- Fix: Filter the catch with `when (callerToken.IsCancellationRequested)` (as `ModelCallExecutor.cs:143` does) so a non-caller OCE falls into the generic catch, which sets `IsConnectivityFailure` before the `finally`; belt-and-braces: treat `seed.Turn.IsFinalized` at `:1700` as "begin a new turn". Keep the partial-usage recording for the caller case.
- Failing test: `WizardIntelligenceProviderFallbackTests.StreamPromptAsync_timeout_on_primary_defers_turn_to_next_candidate` (primary throws `new TaskCanceledException("t", new TimeoutException())` before any token; assert `DiscardedAssistantEntryIds` empty after candidate 1 and the final assistant entry finalized once with candidate B's text).
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-053 · S17-05 · Api/Wizard · The daily budget is not re-evaluated between model calls once the pre-call estimate plateaus, and the fixed 1-hour reservation TTL has no renewal
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V11: the raise-only design is documented (DESIGN §22.2); while context grows each round's rising estimate does trigger `AdjustAsync`, which rechecks committed + outstanding spend; the gap is the plateau case (context trimmed to the window, or no growth) where N rounds can each spend what one admission was sized for; "N× the daily limit" is the plateau worst case, not the typical loop
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/TurnAccountingHandle.cs:138-185` (high-water early return :158-163), `:327` (`ExpiresAt = UtcNow.AddHours(1)`); callers `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:2935,3939`, one-shot `BudgetMonitor.CheckAsync` at `:656,1001`; `Infra/Data/BudgetReservationService.cs:115-190,339`; docs `docs/Arcanum.CHAT-LOOP.md:33`, `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:56,1654`
- Defect: when the estimate does not grow, `EnsureReservationForContextAsync` returns without any budget check, so accumulated actual spend (the `BillableOperations` of earlier rounds) is never compared with the daily limit again; the explicit "operator-owned token or cost policy" stop that CHAT-LOOP:33 and DESIGN:56/§10.7.3 list does not exist for the main loop. The reservation also expires at one hour with no renewal and `ReconcileAsync` filters on `Status = Reserved`, so an hour-plus turn can end with an expired reservation.
- Fix: In the early-return branch still evaluate `committed + outstanding` against the limit at most once per round (cheap read, same non-immediate path as `BudgetMonitor`), failing with `Budget.Exceeded` through the existing context-gate failure; renew `ExpiresAt` per adjust/round. Do not change the "never multiply by call count" rule: the check is of accumulated actual spend. Update CHAT-LOOP:33 and DESIGN §22.2 to state exactly what is checked and when.
- Failing test: `TurnAccountingHandleTests.EnsureReservationForContextAsync_FailsWhenAccumulatedSpendExceedsDailyLimitEvenIfEstimateDidNotGrow` (limit 1.00, record 0.95 actual usage, second call with an equal breakdown; assert `Budget.Exceeded`; red today: `Result.Success()` at the high-water early return).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.CHAT-LOOP.md` "Termination, progress, and cancellation" table row "Explicit policy"; `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §22.2
- Design conflict: none (DESIGN §22.2 documents raise-only; the check addition does not contradict it)

### R-054 · S17-10 · Api/Wizard · Streaming error frames drop the typed error code on budget, guardrail, begin-turn, accounting and spell failures; buffered preserves it
- Verification: Confirmed — V18 (list corrected: `:1722` is the buffered-only branch covered by R-050; `:4199` is not a drop; the HTTP status is already 200 for NDJSON, so the loss is the machine-readable `data` code and OpenAI `type` derivation on `/v1`)
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/WizardIntelligenceProvider.cs:975,996,1005,1042,1140,1749,2357,1794-1795,2067-2068,2140-2141,2408-2409` (frames built without the `Data` argument), mapper default `:506-510` (`frame.Data ?? ErrorCodes.Hub.Error`); `Api/Intelligence/TurnEngine/Projections/IntelligenceEventProjection.cs:183-190`; `Api/OpenAiV1Endpoints.cs` Error case
- Defect: `IntelligenceEvent(Error, message)` is constructed without the third (`Data`) argument at these sites while other frames pass `error.Code`, so streaming clients see `Hub.Error` where the buffered route returns `Budget.Exceeded`, `Hub.SessionTurnBusy`, the guardrail code, `Validation.AttachedFiles` or `Hub.Model`; clients cannot back off or retry correctly on streaming and `/v1` stream error types derive from the same code.
- Fix: Pass `error.Code` as the third argument at every listed site (add an `ErrorFrame(Error)` helper), then make the mapper default loud or keep `Hub.Error` only for genuinely untyped provider failures. `IntelligenceEvent.Data` doubles as the usage/payload carrier on other event types, so set it only on `Error`.
- Failing test: strengthen `WizardIntelligenceProviderTests.StreamPromptAsync_BudgetExceeded_YieldsErrorEventAndSkipsInference` (`:215-236`) with `Assert.Equal(ErrorCodes.Budget.Exceeded, error.Data)` and add `StreamPromptAsync_GuardrailInputBlocked_CarriesGuardrailCode`, `StreamPromptAsync_SessionTurnBusy_CarriesSessionTurnBusyCode`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md` §8 NDJSON `error` frame (state `data` is always the typed code)
- Design conflict: none

### R-055 · S34-02 · Api/Wizard · Nothing exercises the tool-path Sanctum network egress ward or the `run_spell_script`/`read_url` Sanctum preflight
- Verification: Confirmed — V21 (even the first-hop `read_url` Sanctum check and the `run_spell_script` per-root preflight are unpinned)
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/ToolExecutionPipeline.cs:1707-1723,1930-1936,1956-1991,2194-2257,2260-2282`; `Api/Intelligence/Tools/ArcanumReadUrlTool.cs:132`; `Infra/Web/LocalHttpWebProvider.cs:188-189`; tests only `NativeWebResearchProviderTests.cs:453,492`, `WebWorkflowEndpointTests.cs:1159-1211`, `SanctumGuardTests.cs:1069`
- Defect: deleting `RedirectEgressWard = SanctumEgressWardAmbient.Current` (:132), `using IDisposable? egressWard = BeginSanctumEgressWard(...)` (:1723), the `read_url`/`browse_web` case, or the `run_spell_script` case turns no test red; the only link from pipeline to tool is an AsyncLocal. An allowlisted host's 302 off the allow-list inside a strict Sanctum would be followed with no block or `NetworkEgress` breach.
- Fix: Tests only. The ward is published only when `turnContext.Campaign is not null && SanctumEnabled`, so build an enabled-Sanctum `TurnContext` and give `read_url` a recording `IWebResearchProvider`.
- Failing test: `ToolExecutionPipelineSanctumEgressTests.ReadUrl_InStrictSanctum_PublishesWardAndOptionsCarryIt` (assert `LastReadOptions.RedirectEgressWard` non-null, invoke it with an off-allowlist URI → false + one `NetworkEgress` breach), `ReadUrl_OffAllowlistFirstHop_IsDeniedBeforeToolRuns`, `RunSpellScript_ScriptUnderEveryResonantRoot_IsSanctumValidated` (guard fake records `ValidatePathAsync` per root; a denying second root prevents invocation), and negative `ReadUrl_NoSanctum_PublishesNoWard`.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-056 · S34-03 · Api/Wizard · The pin materializer's cross-session attachment guard and file/symbol-range symlink containment have no test
- Verification: Widened (Important retained) — V21: `SecureFileReader`'s O_NOFOLLOW governs only the FINAL component, so for an intermediate directory symlink `:645-656` is the ONLY guard and deleting it is a real escape, not merely an unproven guard
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `Api/Intelligence/SessionContextPinMaterializer.cs:159,389,563-600` (cross-session guard `:570`), `:623-656` (containment `:645-656`); tests `tests/Intelligence/SessionContextPinMaterializerTests.cs:229,285,336,450-500`; pin creation does not check attachment ownership (`Api/Tower/SessionEndpoints.cs:1314-1360`); `Infra/Security/FileHandleIdentity.cs:715-808`
- Defect: mutation A (`if (record is null)` at `:570`) is green because the only Attachment test uses the same session and an image; a session-B pin carrying session-A's attachment GUID would read session-A text into session B's prompt. Mutation B (delete the `link is not null` block) is green because File/SymbolRange tests cover only the lexical `../` escape and the three directory-snapshot symlink tests use `MaterializeDirectory`'s own check; with `<ws>/link -> /outside` and pin `link/secret.txt` the file opens through the directory link.
- Fix: Tests only. The symlink test must use an INTERMEDIATE directory symlink (a final-component file symlink stays green under the mutation because the no-follow open rejects it). Coordinate with R-283 (S34-08: a root reached through a symlink makes all internal links "escape"); do not fix that in a way that opens the traversal case.
- Failing test: `SessionContextPinMaterializerTests.Attachment_pin_for_another_sessions_attachment_reports_Missing_without_reading_bytes` (record with a different `SessionId`, `readBytes` spy never called; also the logical-key lookup variant) and `File_pin_through_directory_symlink_to_outside_workspace_is_Unsafe_and_content_not_read` (+ identical `SymbolRange` case `link/secret.txt:1-1`); skip on Windows as the existing symlink tests do.
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-057 · S19-01 / V-S19-01 · Api/Endpoints · About 50 bound-body routes bypass `ApiRequestJson`; malformed/oversize/wrong-type bodies get a bare 400/413/415 in production and a logged 500 `Hub.Unhandled` in Development (and `API.md:412` overstates the `toolPolicy` contract)
- Verification: Confirmed (citations corrected) — V8: with `RouteHandlerOptions.ThrowOnBadRequest` false (default outside Development) the generated binder writes an empty 400/415; in Development it defaults true and `ArcanumExceptionHandler` has no `BadHttpRequestException` arm; `API.md:327` is scoped to routes that read the body themselves and `:867` already says bound routes get the empty framework response, so the contract actually false for bound routes is `API.md:412` (invalid `toolPolicy` "surfacing as 400 Validation.InvalidBody on the native routes" for Spell/Prompt execute)
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Api/Tower/PromptEndpoints.cs:140,786-788`; `Api/Tower/SessionEndpoints.cs:53`; `Api/Data/DataRetentionEndpoints.cs:82,133,168,237,266,312,388`; `Api/Perception/PerceptionEndpoints.cs:96`; `Api/Mcp/DiagnosticMcpInvocationEndpoints.cs:37`; `Api/Conclave/ApprenticeEndpoints.cs:88,249,279,309`; `Api/Tower/CovenantMutationEndpoints.cs:43-179`; `Api/Tower/CampaignEndpoints.cs:~455`; `Api/Tower/SpellExecutionEndpoints.cs`; `Api/OpenAiV1FilesEndpoints.cs:58`; `Api/Middleware/ArcanumExceptionHandler.cs:35-72,131-162`; `Api/ApiRequestJson.cs:64-70`; `RetroDownfall.Arcanum.Api.csproj:7` (RDG); no `ThrowOnBadRequest` anywhere under `src/`
- Defect: roughly 50 handlers still bind the body as a record parameter, so clients get a body-less 400/413/415 instead of the `ApiResponse` envelope (`Validation.InvalidBody`/`UnsupportedMediaType`/`BodyTooLarge`), and the DevHost (`DOTNET_ENVIRONMENT=Development`) reports client mistakes as server faults (500). `/v1/files` multipart above the 513 MiB ceiling fails the same way.
- Fix: Add a `BadHttpRequestException` arm to `ArcanumExceptionHandler` that reuses `ApiRequestJson.UnreadableBodyResult` (and the OpenAI result on `/v1`, returning false when `Response.HasStarted`) and set `RouteHandlerOptions.ThrowOnBadRequest = true` in `AddArcanumApiServices` so binder failures reach the handler in every environment; or convert the high-traffic routes (Spell/Prompt execute move to `ForRawBody`) to `ApiRequestJson.ReadAsync` as the Saga curation routes did. Qualify `API.md:412` to name the three routes.
- Failing test: `ApiWireContractTests.PostPrompts_MalformedJson_And_NonJsonContentType_ReturnEnvelopedErrors` (Production-like and Development factories; `{not-json` and `text/plain`; assert 400/415 + `ApiResponse` body with the documented code), `ArcanumExceptionHandlerTests.TryHandleAsync_BadHttpRequestException_413_ReturnsBodyTooLargeEnvelope`, `ApiWireContractTests.SpellExecute_UnknownToolPolicy_Returns_InvalidBodyEnvelope`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md` §8.1 (:327, :867), error table, and :412
- Design conflict: none

### R-058 · S19-04 · Api/Endpoints · The Chronicle and Session SSE routes add a second silent `DropOldest` buffer, so the documented `eventsDropped` marker / log warning never fires for a slow client
- Verification: Confirmed — V8
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Conclave/ApprenticeEndpoints.cs:~399-433,557-577`; `Api/Tower/SessionEndpoints.cs:~1655-1670,2685-2704`; `Infra/Hosting/ChronicleHub.cs:~35-58`; consumers `Cli/UX/WatchEventView.cs:146`, `TheForge.Ux/.../ChronicleEntryViewModel.cs:25`; docs `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:965,3552`
- Defect: the pump drains the hub channel as fast as it can into a route-local `Channel.CreateBounded(..., DropOldest)`; `WriteAsync` on a `DropOldest` channel never waits and never reports a drop, so the hub's channel never fills and `ChronicleHub` never synthesizes `EventsDropped`, while the route-local channel (where a slow reader overflows) drops oldest frames with no marker and no log. Reconnect logic keying on `eventsDropped` never triggers.
- Fix: Drop the second buffer (stream straight from the hub reader through `SseStreamWriter.StreamAsync`), or use `TryWrite` and on failure emit an `EventsDropped` event plus a Warning log (or `BoundedChannelFullMode.Wait` with a timeout, so the stall reaches the hub's own overflow path). Keep the hub marker logic.
- Failing test: `ApprenticeEndpointTests.Chronicle_slow_reader_receives_eventsDropped_marker` (publish more than `EventBusChannelCapacity` events while the client does not read, then read and assert an `eventsDropped` frame) plus a session twin asserting the warning log.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` :965 and :3552
- Design conflict: none

### R-059 · S19-06 / S26-05 · Api/Endpoints · Campaign import reads `.arcanum/campaign.json` with an unbounded, symlink-following `File.ReadAllTextAsync`
- Verification: Confirmed — V8 (S26-05 filed Minor/Plausible; same code)
- Category: security
- Location: `Api/Tower/CampaignEndpoints.cs:451-460,~481-489` (`File.Exists` + `File.ReadAllTextAsync(diskPath, ctx.RequestAborted)`); contrast capped readers `Infra/Security/SecureFileReader.cs`, `Core/Configuration/CodexPathPolicy.cs` `ReadCappedAsync`; threat-model statement `tests/Api/CodexEndpointTests.cs:11-13`
- Defect: `campaign.Path` is by design an operator-cloned repo; `.arcanum/campaign.json` there can be a symlink to `/dev/zero` (unbounded allocation, host OOM), a FIFO (the open blocks forever; `File.Exists` is true) or any huge file. A parse failure on a symlink to a secret file returns a generic message, so this is denial of service, not read disclosure. Every other workspace file read in `src/` goes through a capped no-follow reader.
- Fix: Read through the capped, no-follow, regular-file-only reader used for Codex files with a code-owned size cap high enough for legitimate export bundles with embedded spells/prompts; map refusal to `Campaign.ImportFailed`; drop the `File.Exists` pre-check.
- Failing test: `CampaignEndpointTests.Import_from_disk_refuses_a_symlinked_or_oversized_campaign_json` (symlink to a large/outside file and an oversized regular file; assert 400 `Campaign.ImportFailed` and the target not read to the end).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md` campaign import section (state the on-disk bundle size cap)
- Design conflict: none

### R-060 · S19-07 · Api/Endpoints · `ArcanumJsonContext` registration is only proven for hand-listed types; the JIT test host silently falls back to reflection for any missed `Results.Ok(ApiResponse<T>)` payload
- Verification: Confirmed (no present defect; missing guard) — V8
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `tests/Api/Serialization/ArcanumJsonContextCompletenessTests.cs:87,144`; `Api/ApiBootstrapper.cs:373` (`TypeInfoResolverChain.Insert(0, ArcanumJsonContext.Default)` leaves the default resolver); `ApiSurfaceContractTests.cs:375,765,813`; `scripts/verify-shipping-publish.sh` (in-process smoke, visits no routes)
- Defect: removing `[JsonSerializable(typeof(ApiResponse<WardDto>))]` leaves every integration test green because the JIT test host keeps reflection JSON enabled (`JsonSerializerIsReflectionEnabledByDefault` set nowhere); the completeness theories assert only types they list, and the Cli has an analogous `CliJsonContextCoverageTests` but the Api has no route-walking equivalent, so the cheapest AGENTS.md rule-1 regression reaches users in the shipped AOT binary.
- Fix: Set `<JsonSerializerIsReflectionEnabledByDefault>false</JsonSerializerIsReflectionEnabledByDefault>` in `RetroDownfall.Arcanum.Tests.csproj`, or add a reflection test over `EndpointDataSource` handler return types. Hazard: the switch flips every test serializing a non-registered type (`A2AParkedSendingTests.cs:743`, `A2AServerCapabilityTests.cs:463` use `DefaultJsonTypeInfoResolver`); expect a first-run cleanup.
- Failing test: `ArcanumJsonContextCompletenessTests.Every_route_response_type_is_source_generated` (enumerate `EndpointDataSource` endpoint metadata/handler return types and assert `ArcanumJsonContext.Default.GetTypeInfo(t) != null`; red only after a registration is removed).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §9 (AOT discipline gates)
- Design conflict: none

### R-061 · S33-01 · Api/Endpoints · `POST /api/prompts/{id}/test` lets the caller choose the containment root for `codexPath`, bypassing the workspace allowlist
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V20: the bypassed allowlist is `Arcanum:Security:SpellWorkspaceRoots` (`SpellWorkspaceResolver.cs:14-34,112-127`; default `[]`, so empty denies on a stock install), not `CampaignRoots`; read-only, existing regular files only, size capped (`EffectiveCodexMaxSizeBytes` 1 KiB–1 MiB), authenticated
- Category: security
- Location: `Api/Tower/PromptEndpoints.cs:422,469,473-502,509,522-536,573,599-606`; `Core/Configuration/CodexPathPolicy.cs:29-84`; `Api/Spells/SpellWorkspaceResolver.cs`; `docs/Arcanum.API.md:197`
- Defect: for a global prompt (or a deleted/blank-path Campaign) the only containment root is the request's own `workingDirectory`, never run through `SpellWorkspaceResolver`/`EnforceAllowedRoots`; `{"workingDirectory":"/","codexPath":"/Users/x/.ssh/config"}` passes both containment checks and the file is read and echoed in `assembled`. Prompt execute, spell execute and `ping` all enforce the allowlist; `/test` does not (while `/execute` with a `workingDirectory` is refused on a stock install). The same unvalidated directory is the MCP workspace partition key (`:573`); workspace MCP config loading is separately gated by trust, so not rated code execution.
- Fix: Before the codex block call `SpellWorkspaceResolver.Resolve(workingDirectory)` (or `ResolveRequired`) and use its result as `containmentRoot` and for `GetAvailableToolsAsync`; map failure via `ArcanumErrorMapper.ResolveStatusCode` (`Spell.InvalidWorkspace`/`Spell.PathNotAllowed`). Keep Campaign-path precedence. Do not resolve when `codexPath` is absent and `workingDirectory` is blank, or `/test` without codex starts failing on installs with empty roots; decide whether an unlisted directory should merely skip codex/tools instead of failing.
- Failing test: `PromptTestEndpointTests.Test_with_codexPath_under_a_workingDirectory_outside_the_configured_roots_is_refused` (global prompt, temp file `T/secret.txt` with a sentinel, `SpellWorkspaceRoots=[]`, POST `{"workingDirectory":"T","codexPath":"T/secret.txt"}`; assert 403 `Spell.PathNotAllowed` and no sentinel in the body; red today: 200 + sentinel). No HTTP test of `codexPath` on this route exists.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md:197` (state that `workingDirectory` must satisfy the workspace allowlist; the doc currently documents the working directory as the root)
- Design conflict: none (API.md:197 documents the working-directory-as-root; this is a gap against the allowlist stance, not a sanctioned behavior)

### R-062 · S33-03 · Api/Endpoints · `PUT /api/prompts/{id}` turns a unique-index violation into 500 `Hub.Unhandled`; no name/version validation on update (create/clone FK and race variants Plausible)
- Verification: Confirmed (update path); create/clone variants Plausible — V20
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Tower/PromptEndpoints.cs:152-186,199,223-231,285,323-357`; `Infra/Repositories/PromptRepository.cs:154-174` (no constraint mapping); `Infra/Data/Schema/Tables/Prompts.sql:18,23,25`; `Api/Middleware/ArcanumExceptionHandler.cs:128-175`; contrast `CampaignRepository.AddAsync`
- Defect: create and clone pre-check `GetByNameAndVersionAsync` but update assigns the new Name/Version with no lookup, so renaming a prompt onto an existing `(name, version, campaign)` is a deterministic 500 with an Error stack trace and the documented `Prompt.DuplicateVersion` is unreachable there; `request.Name = "  "` is accepted and stored as `""` (create rejects blank); create/clone accept any `CampaignId` (FK 500) and lose the check-then-insert race to the same 500. Transaction rolls back, no data lost.
- Fix: In update trim and reject blank `Name`/`Version` (`Prompt.InvalidName`/`InvalidVersion`); when either changes call `GetByNameAndVersionAsync(name, version, existing.CampaignId)` and reject when the hit's `Id != id` with `Prompt.DuplicateVersion` (exclude the same row or a no-op rename self-collides); add a repository `DbUpdateException`/`SqliteException` code 19 mapping (mirror `CampaignRepository.AddAsync`) so races also land on 400; verify the campaign exists on create/clone (`Campaign.NotFound`).
- Failing test: `PromptEndpointTests.Update_renaming_to_an_existing_name_and_version_answers_400_DuplicateVersion` (red today: 500 `Hub.Unhandled`), `Update_with_blank_name_answers_400_InvalidName`, `Create_with_an_unknown_campaignId_answers_404`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md` :194 (PUT: list `Prompt.DuplicateVersion`, `Prompt.InvalidName`)
- Design conflict: none

### R-063 · S33-04 · Api/Endpoints · `POST /api/campaigns/{id}/import` with `strategy:"replace"` deletes prompts one by one before per-prompt validity is known, and is not atomic
- Verification: Confirmed — V20 (and the documented guarantee is false for prompt-shape errors)
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Api/Tower/CampaignEndpoints.cs:451,475,514-557,558-566,568-575,577-617`; `Api/Tower/PromptMapping.cs:82-148`; `Infra/Repositories/PromptRepository.cs:176-186`; test `tests/Api/Tower/CampaignEndpointTests.cs:74-105`; docs `docs/Arcanum.API.md:177`
- Defect: pre-sweep validation never looks at prompt members, so a bundle whose prompt lacks `template` (or blank name/version, or duplicate name+version inside the bundle) deletes every existing Campaign prompt, `ImportAsync` returns `Prompt.InvalidRequest` into `warnings`, and the route answers 200 "Imported 0 prompts". Each delete and add is its own autocommitted statement on `ctx.RequestAborted`, so a disconnect leaves a partial set; only the first 10,000 prompts are listed; any `strategy` other than the literal `"replace"` silently behaves as merge. `API.md:177` ("validated in full before anything is written") is false for per-prompt shape.
- Fix: Validate every prompt entry (extract `PromptImportHelper.Validate`, detect in-bundle duplicate `(name,version)`) before the sweep and return `ImportFailed`; reject any `strategy` other than `merge`/`replace` (case-insensitive) with 400; add `IPromptRepository.ReplaceCampaignPromptsAsync(campaignId, prompts, ct)` doing delete + all adds in one transaction (EF lane for adds, direct SQL delete on the same admitted connection per AGENTS.md rule 1) with `CancellationToken.None` after the first delete. The unique index `IX_Prompts_Name_Version_Campaign` requires delete-then-add inside one transaction, and the existing-duplicate pre-check must stay out of the replace path.
- Failing test: `CampaignEndpointTests.ImportCampaign_replace_with_one_prompt_lacking_template_rejects_without_deleting_prompts` (existing prompt remains, 400 `Campaign.ImportFailed`; red today: 200 and the prompt is gone) and `ImportCampaign_unknown_strategy_is_400`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md:177` (per-prompt validation precedes the sweep; list accepted `strategy` values)
- Design conflict: none

### R-064 · S33-05 · Api/Endpoints · `POST /api/sessions/divine` applies the campaign/status filters after the vector top-K, so filtered requests silently under-return or return nothing
- Verification: Confirmed — V20
- Category: correctness
