- Location: `Api/Tower/SessionDivinationEndpoints.cs:47,100-127,137-148,251-264,312-315`; `Infra/Weave/DivinationService.cs:362-364` (KNN `LIMIT @maxResults` before any join); `Core/Configuration/ArcanumSettingClamps.cs:351` (limit clamp 1–50); test `tests/Api/Tower/SessionDivinationEndpointTests.cs:189-240`; docs `docs/Arcanum.API.md:83`
- Defect: the vector search returns the globally nearest `limit` entries first and the campaign/status predicate (default `status=active`) drops non-matching hits afterwards, so `?campaignId=X` can return zero hits although X holds close matches ranked below the global cutoff, and archived sessions consume slots; `hasMore` is hard-coded false. `Divine_FiltersByCampaignAndStatus` seeds three identical-vector entries within the default limit, so pre- and post-filtering are indistinguishable.
- Fix: Over-fetch iteratively: request `limit * k` (capped, e.g. 500) from `DivinationService.SearchAsync`, filter, repeat doubling until `limit` survivors or the KNN result is smaller than requested; set `HasMore` truthfully (or push the filter into the vec query once the `entry_embeddings_vec` auxiliary/partition columns are checked). Over-fetch must keep the same `similarityThreshold`, which `SearchAsync` applies inside the KNN.
- Failing test: `SessionDivinationEndpointTests.Divine_FilteredByCampaign_returns_matches_ranked_below_the_global_cutoff` (seed `limit+1` closer entries `[1,0,0]` in another campaign and one farther `[0.9,0.1,0]` in the target; request `limit:3, CampaignId:target`; red today: empty results).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.API.md:83`
- Design conflict: none

### R-065 · S20-01 · Cli · `arcanum run -p` on a terminal still blocks on a "Prompt:" line read
- Verification: Confirmed — V9 (only `-p` is documented headless; the `--json` half is a design extension, not a documented guarantee)
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Cli/Commands/RunInputReader.cs:75-96`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliApplicationFactory.cs:256`; `Cli/Commands/RunCommand.cs:137-140,188-193,203-209`; `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:26`; test gap `tests/Cli/RunInputReaderTests.cs:117-119`
- Defect: when stdin is a real TTY and neither a positional instruction nor `--with` is present, the reader writes `Prompt:` and awaits `ReadLineAsync`; it never consults `Print` or `Json` (it has no `ICliInvocationContext`), unlike `RunCommand`, `CliEnvironment`, `ConfirmationPrompt` and `SensitiveValueInput`. The documented contract is that `-p` "behaves like a redirected one" so a scripted invocation cannot stall; a CI wrapper that allocates a pty and omits the prompt words hangs instead of exiting 2.
- Fix: Inject `ICliInvocationContext` (or read `CliInvocationContext.Current`) into `RunInputReader`; when `Print` (and, if chosen, `Json`) is set and `!redirected`, skip the prompt and return the empty `Success("")` result that `RunCommand.cs:203-209` turns into exit 2. Keep the `internal` 3-arg test constructor.
- Failing test: `RunInputReaderTests.ReadAsync_does_not_prompt_when_print_is_set_on_a_true_tty` (`inputRedirected: () => false`, `Print=true`, no instruction; assert the injected line is not consumed and no `Prompt:` diagnostic written; theory over `Json` if adopted).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md` run section (state that `--print` never prompts)
- Design conflict: none

### R-066 · S20-02 / S32-01 · Cli · `session list`, `campaign list`, `campaign prompts`, `prompt list`, `apprentice list` and `saga list` silently show only the first server page
- Verification: Confirmed — V9 (S20-02) and V15 (S32-01); wider than filed for `campaign prompts` and `prompt list`, which expose no limit/offset knob at all; `saga list` returns a bare array with no `HasMore`
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Cli/Commands/Tower/SessionCommands.cs:38-117`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.Spells.cs:391,405-415`; `Core/Tower/SessionDtos.cs:77-80`; `Cli/Commands/Tower/CampaignCommands.cs:167-176,559-570`; `Cli/Commands/Tower/PromptCommands.cs:58-69`; `Cli/Commands/Conclave/ApprenticeCommands.cs:151-162`; `Cli/Commands/Tower/SagaCommands.cs:52-65`; `Cli/Services/ArcanumApiClient.cs:1022-1048,2535-2540,2692-2706,3144-3158,3399-3411,3753-3765`; server defaults `Infra/Repositories/CampaignRepository.cs:20,97`, `PromptRepository.cs:14,114`, `ApprenticeRepository.cs:16,51`, `Api/Tower/SagaEndpoints.cs:30,70`; contrast `CampaignCommands.cs:663-737`, `WorkspaceCommands.cs:258-314,793-845`
- Defect: `ListPageResult<T>`/`SessionQueryResult` carry `HasMore` and `NextOffset`/`NextBeforeUpdatedAt`, but these handlers read neither, so installations with more than one page (100 rows) get a table or `--json` array that looks complete, on lists operators use for delete/cancel decisions. Command Reference lines 56-72 and the Lore row (:500) state that a bound "must expose or automatically follow its continuation".
- Fix: Follow `NextOffset`/`NextBeforeUpdatedAt` to exhaustion with a repeated-token no-progress fault (copy `WorkspaceCommands.GetAllCampaignsAsync`), or add a cursor option and print the "more results" hint exactly like `CampaignCommands.Sessions`; `--limit` keeps meaning "one page" if retained. `saga list` needs a server change first (page type with `HasMore`) or the CLI must request `limit+1`. `--json` currently wraps text (see R-407), so do not change the machine surface in the same commit without documenting it.
- Failing test: `SessionManagementCommandTests.SessionList_follows_or_reports_continuation_when_the_host_reports_more` (page 1 `HasMore=true`, page 2 empty; assert the page-2 request carries `beforeUpdatedAt`), `CampaignCommandTests.List_follows_hasMore_until_exhausted`, and equivalents in `PromptCommandTests`, `ApprenticeCommandTests`; `SagaCommandTests.List_reports_when_a_prefix_was_shown`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md` rows for `session list`, `campaign list`, `campaign prompts`, `prompt list`, `apprentice list`, `saga list`
- Design conflict: none

### R-067 · S20-04 · Cli · `web search|browse|research --save` validates the destination only after the work is done, and a failed save discards the result
- Verification: Confirmed — V9
- Category: usability
- Location: `Cli/Commands/WebWorkflowCommands.cs:81-86,153-160,355-362,442-500` (directory check ~:463, `File.Move(..., overwrite:true)` :486); `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.Web.cs:203,227`
- Defect: the `--save` directory is checked inside `SaveAsync` after the (billed, possibly unbounded) call returns, and on failure the method returns 1 before `WritePayload`/`WriteJson` runs, so a typo in the directory loses the whole synthesized answer. Command Reference lines 927/941/960 promise only "atomically save the final Markdown content", no ordering.
- Fix: Resolve and validate the destination (directory exists, writable) before sending the request; on a late save failure still emit the result, then return the failure code. Keep the temp-file-and-move write; validation must not create the file.
- Failing test: `WebWorkflowCommandTests.Research_with_unwritable_save_path_fails_before_calling_the_host` (zero host requests, exit 2) and `..._still_prints_the_answer_when_the_late_save_fails`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md` `web research` row (`--save` ordering)
- Design conflict: none

### R-068 · S20-06 / S32-03 · Cli · `Connection.*` failures return exit 1 in most command families and in ten Covenant verbs, 3 in a few
- Verification: Confirmed — V9 (no single fix site: failures arrive as `Result` and each verb converts to an int locally) and V15 (S32-03)
- Category: convention
- Location: generic-1 sinks: `Cli/Commands/AttachmentCommands.cs:940-947`, `FileBatchCommands.cs:647-654`, `WebWorkflowCommands.cs:578-585`, `DataRetentionCommands.cs:592-599`, `ToolCommands.cs:220-231`, `Configuration/ResourceBrowseCommands.cs:722-733`, `Configuration/WorkspaceCommands.cs:871-878`, `OperationCommands.cs:20-21,56-57,101-102,122-123`, `Daemon/DaemonCommands.cs:90-95,189-194,258-263` (only Jobs/Initiative/Alert are API-backed; install/uninstall/status fail on local daemon-manager results), `ProvingGrounds/TrialCommands.cs:115-120`, `Wards/WardCommands.cs:24-28,74-86,154-170`, `Conclave/ConclaveCommands.cs:150-176` (`Status` at :33-42 classifies), `ServeCommand.cs:42-50`, `Tower/SagaCommands.cs:56-61,167-172,245-250,272-277`, `Configuration/ModelProviderCommands.cs:26-30,113-117`; Covenant `Tower/CovenantCommands.cs:106,165,251,321,413,532,625,829,1070,1199` (all `Fail(.., CliExitCode.GenericError)`; classifying lines `:1249,1280,1351,1468`; `Fail` at `:1521-1539`); classifier `Commands/CliFailureExit.cs:24-27`; client `Services/ArcanumApiClient.cs:94-102,4064-4065`
- Defect: AGENTS.md defines exit 3 as "network error"; `CliFailureExit` exists because only five sites classified it and was rolled out to Tower/Lore/Apprentice/Memory only, so the same fault gives `conclave status` 3 vs `conclave dispatch` 1, `session show` 3 vs `attachment list` 1, and `covenant erase|release` 3 vs the ten other Covenant verbs 1 (also `exitCode:1` in the `--json` envelope). Only `run`/`ask`/Command Center call `EnsureRunningAsync`, so every other verb really sees the bare connection failure; the thrown-`HttpRequestException` net in `CliFailureMapper` never catches them because the client converts transport failures into `Result`. Command Reference :67 hedges the contract.
- Fix: Route every failed host `Result` through `CliFailureExit.ExitCode`/`Annotate`: change the eight private `WriteError(Error)` helpers' `return 1` to `return CliFailureExit.ExitCode(error)` (one line each) and swap the ~15 inline `return 1`/`GenericError` sites (Daemon API verbs, Trial, Ward, Conclave.Render, Serve quit, Saga, ModelProvider); in `CovenantCommands` give `Fail(Error)` an overload computing the code and keep the two-argument overload for client-side validation (`RevisionConflict`, `CurationRevisionConflict`). Do not change the output channel (`CliErrorOutput.WriteMarkupLine` vs `dispatcher.WriteDiagnostic`); check existing `CovenantCommandTests` assertions of exit 1 are not `Connection.*` cases.
- Failing test: `ConnectionFailureExitCodeTests` (theory over the listed verbs: handler throws `HttpRequestException`/returns a `Connection` error; assert exit 3 and empty stdout; model on `BudgetAndConclaveExitCodeTests`) and `CovenantCommandTests.Host_unreachable_exits_3_for_every_verb` (set, correct, retire, pin, unpin, mask, unmask, list, search, show, show --history; under `--json` `exitCode:3`).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md` "Output and exit behavior" row `3` (drop the hedge at :67) and Covenant rows :706-715
- Design conflict: none

### R-069 · S21-03 · Cli · `arcanum doctor` throws away the per-component verdicts of a host that answers 503 and then says "its own health report names the failing components"
- Verification: Confirmed (remedy sub-claim narrowed) — V12: the `lore` remedy is a documented, contract-test-pinned choice (`Command.Reference.md:440`, `DoctorRemedyCommandContractTests`)
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Cli/Services/ArcanumHealthProbe.cs:61,136-161`; `Cli/Diagnostics/HostHealthDiagnostics.cs:32-57,71-93,128-165`; `Api/Health/HealthEndpoints.cs:40-44` (503 + full `HealthReportDto`); `Cli/Services/ArcanumApiClient.Watch.cs:589-597` (`MapHealthReport` already accepts the 503 body); `Cli/Diagnostics/DoctorDiagnosticsRegistration.cs:76`; test `tests/Cli/HostHealthDiagnosticsTests.cs:18-95`
- Defect: the probe reads components only when `IsSuccessStatusCode`; overall Unhealthy is exactly when the endpoint answers 503, so the unhealthy-component formatting branch is effectively unreachable and `Describe` prints only "HTTP 503" and points at `arcanum lore`, dropping the host's own verdicts in the state that matters most (documented behavior "relayed rather than re-implemented", `Command.Reference.md:440`). Only Unauthorized, UnexpectedResponder and no-answer are tested.
- Fix: In `ProbeAuthenticatedAsync` call `TryReadComponentsAsync(response, cts.Token)` for 503 too and pass `components` into the `UnhealthyStatus` result; route `UnhealthyStatus`-with-components through the existing unhealthy/degraded formatter inside the same `cts.Token` and `IOException`/`HttpRequestException` catches; keep the no-components fallback for a foreign 503 body. Changing the remedy command means changing the doc table and `DoctorRemedyCommandContractTests` together.
- Failing test: `HostHealthDiagnosticsTests.A_503_with_components_names_the_failing_components` (503 + `ApiResponse<HealthReportDto>` with one Unhealthy component `Grimoire`; assert detail contains `Grimoire=unhealthy` and outcome `Unhealthy`).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md` (doctor section, only if the remedy text changes)
- Design conflict: none

### R-070 · S22-01 · Cli · Staged-attachment build in the Command Center is uncancellable, sits outside the turn's try/finally, and the FIFO test pins only half of the contract
- Verification: Widened (Important retained) — V9: the same unguarded `File.ReadAllText` also applies to character devices (`/dev/zero`: length 0, unbounded read); trigger is operator-supplied (`@path`, `/attach`), not model-supplied
- Category: reliability
- Location: `Cli/CommandCenter/CommandCenterChatRunner.cs:35-72,51-58,99,358-410`; `Cli/CommandCenter/CommandCenterTurnAttachmentBuilder.cs:60-70,144-150`; `Cli/CommandCenter/CommandCenterHost.cs:257,1891-1942`; test `tests/Cli/CommandCenter/CommandCenterAttachmentTests.cs:221-257`
- Defect: `Task.Run(Build, ct)` honours `ct` only until the delegate starts; once `File.ReadAllText` blocks in `open(O_RDONLY)` on a FIFO (`File.Exists` true, `FileInfo.Length` 0 so the size guard passes) Ctrl+C cannot interrupt it, `HandleSubmitAsync`'s `finally` (`EndTurn`, CTS dispose) is never reached, `state.Generating` stays true and every later submit says "Already generating" with `ThinkingActive` also stuck. Everything before the `try` at :99 (the `Task.Run`, log appends, cancellable `WriteAsync`) is outside the cleanup, so any non-IO exception from `Build` (e.g. `ArgumentException` from `Path.GetFullPath` on a pre-staged `/attach` path) or an OCE from :57/:71 leaves `ThinkingActive` true. The documented "Ctrl+C returns to the composer" (Command Reference :68) is false for this input.
- Fix: Reject non-regular files before staging (`FileInfo.Attributes`/open then check `FileStream.CanSeek`), make `Build` cooperative with a bounded `ReadAsync(ct)` and await with `.WaitAsync(cancellationToken)`, and move lines 35-72 inside the `try` so the `finally` always clears `ThinkingActive` and completes the entries. Update `A_turn_yields_before_it_reads_a_staged_attachment`, which will hang once non-regular files are rejected (`await File.WriteAllTextAsync(fifo, ...)` blocks in `open(O_WRONLY)` with no timeout): assert the rejection status line instead.
- Failing test: `CommandCenterTurnStartThreadingTests.A_cancelled_turn_with_a_staged_fifo_returns_and_clears_thinking` (mkfifo, `RunTurnAsync("@trace.log", ..., cts.Token)`, `cts.Cancel()`; assert `await run.WaitAsync(5s)` completes with no writer, `state.ThinkingActive == false`, no entry `Streaming`).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` Command Center turn lifecycle/cancellation section (staging is cancellable and rejects non-regular files)
- Design conflict: none

### R-071 · S22-02 · Cli · Model/tool text reaches the Terminal.Gui transcript and the Spectre/raw stdout paths without control-character stripping
- Verification: Plausible — V9 (absence of filtering confirmed; whether Terminal.Gui 2.4.17 neutralises ESC/C0 before the terminal could not be established)
- Category: security
- Location: `Cli/CommandCenter/SessionLogBuffer.cs:411-415,440,504-523`; `Cli/UX/EphemeralReasoningRenderer.cs:121`; `Cli/UX/MarkdigSpectreRenderer.cs:186,206`; `Cli/Commands/AskCommand.cs:385`; only sanitizer `Cli/CommandCenter/IncantationFormatter.cs:240-294`; path `Cli/CommandCenter/CommandCenterChatRunner.cs:159-160,87-88`, `CommandCenterWindow.cs:2064`
- Defect: only the Incantations pane runs `IncantationFormatter.Sanitize`; Transcript (assistant, reasoning, tool-error and status text), the reasoning panel and `ask`'s raw stdout stream carry ESC, other C0 controls and lone `\r` untouched. Model output is attacker-influenced (prompt injection via fetched pages/files), so OSC 52 clipboard writes, title changes or cursor motion are possible on any terminal/driver that passes them through.
- Fix: Sanitize once at the `SessionLogBuffer` append boundary (strip C0 except `\n`, expand `\t`, strip `0x7F` and `0x80-0x9F`, reusing `IncantationFormatter.Sanitize` semantics), not at `FormatEntry`, so the wrap cache keyed on `ReferenceEquals(cached.SourceText, entry.Text)` stays effective; separately sanitize the reasoning panel only when stdout is a TTY. Do not bundle `AskCommand`'s raw `Console.Out.Write(chunk)`: redirected stdout must stay byte-exact.
- Failing test: `CommandCenterTranscriptRenderingTests.Escape_sequences_in_streamed_assistant_text_never_reach_the_transcript_lines` (append `"hi\u001b]52;c;AAAA\u0007\u001b[31mred"` as an Assistant entry, `CopyLinesTo`, assert no line contains `\u001b` or `\u0007`) and `CliReasoningRenderingTests.Reasoning_panel_strips_control_characters`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` Command Center rendering / CLI output safety (untrusted model text is control-stripped)
- Design conflict: none

### R-072 · S32-02 · Cli · `spell validate` exits 0 when the spell is invalid
- Verification: Confirmed — V15
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Cli/Commands/Tower/SpellCommands.cs:453-510` (`validation.IsValid` only picks the panel colour; `return 0;` :510); `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.Spells.cs:99,104`; `Cli/Services/ArcanumApiClient.cs:2966-2980`; `Infra/Intelligence/Spells/SpellRepository.cs:406,452`; docs `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:741`, exit table :62-68
- Defect: the host reports a failed validation as a successful `Result` carrying `IsValid=false`, and the CLI never maps it to a status: a red "Invalid" panel then `CliExitCode.Success`, so `arcanum spell validate x` can never fail in CI; there is also no JSON branch. No `Validate` test exists. The exit-code table documents only "a completed Trial that did not pass" as exit 1, so exit 1 is a doc addition, not a contract change.
- Fix: Return `(int)CliExitCode.GenericError` when `!IsValid` after printing; under `--json` emit `SpellValidationResultDto` (already on `ArcanumJsonContext`) as the one document. Update Reference :741 and the exit-table row `1` ("a completed validation that did not pass"). TheForge.Ux uses the endpoint, not the CLI.
- Failing test: `SpellCommandTests.Validate_returns_nonzero_when_the_host_reports_the_spell_invalid` (`IsValid=false, Errors=["x"]`; assert exit 1 and "x" in output) plus `Validate_returns_zero_for_a_valid_spell`.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:741` and the exit-code table
- Design conflict: none

### R-073 · S32-04 · Cli · `mcp list|show|start|stop|restart|tools --workspace` compares a raw string to `WorkingDirectory`: IDs and names never match, and global servers are excluded
- Verification: Confirmed — V15
- Category: correctness
- Location: `Cli/Commands/Configuration/ResourceBrowseCommands.cs:51-62,580-593,710-720`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.Core.cs:489,510,542,584,623`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.cs:199-224` (`ActiveWorkspace` returns the explicit string or saved `WorkspacePath` verbatim); doc `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:889-897`
- Defect: `McpCommands` takes no `ICliResourceCatalog`, so `--workspace myproj` is compared ordinally to `server.WorkingDirectory.Trim()` and yields an empty table with exit 0; the reference documents "Workspace ID, name, or server path; defaults to saved context or current-path detection" (detection is not implemented for `mcp`). Because `ActiveWorkspace` also injects the saved `use workspace` path, the filtered verbs silently hide every global server (empty working directory). `mcp start|stop` select from the same filtered list; `reload`/`invoke` send the string to the server instead. No test covers `mcp list --workspace`.
- Fix: Resolve the selector through `ICliResourceCatalog.SelectWorkspaceAsync` to a server path (as `SpellCommands` does), canonicalise, and filter to "that workspace OR global" (or document the global exclusion); keep `mcp trust <workspace>` and `reload` consistent with it.
- Failing test: `McpToolCommandTests.Mcp_list_workspace_accepts_a_workspace_name_and_keeps_global_servers` (registry `proj -> /srv/proj`; one global and one `/srv/proj` server; `mcp list --workspace proj` shows both; zero rows today).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:889-896`
- Design conflict: none

### R-074 · S32-05 · Cli · `mcp invoke` appends a text summary line to stdout after the raw JSON result, and the test pins it
- Verification: Narrowed (Important retained) — V15: under `--json` the output is wrapped as `{output, exitCode}`; the broken contract is the plain-redirect case, the documented use
- Category: convention
- Location: `Cli/Commands/Configuration/ResourceBrowseCommands.cs:472-476`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliApplicationFactory.cs:600-615`; `Cli/Commands/CliErrorOutput.cs` (AnsiConsole built over `Console.Out`); `Cli/Infrastructure/CliCommandTree.Core.cs:623`; test `tests/Cli/McpToolCommandTests.cs:255-321` (`Assert.Contains("12", result.Output)` at :320); contrast `ToolCommands.cs:163`; docs `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:73,897`
- Defect: `arcanum mcp invoke tool '{}' > result.json` yields the JSON document followed by `Diagnostic MCP: tool on server; 12ms; truncated: no.`, which no JSON parser accepts, contradicting "Structured stdout is never mixed with diagnostics"; `truncated: yes` is the only signal that the result was cut and it is lost to anyone redirecting stdout. The existing test pins the defect: moving the line to stderr turns it red.
- Fix: Send the summary through `CliErrorOutput.WriteMarkupLine` (stderr) like `tool invoke`; under `--json` emit a structured payload (`durationMs`, `truncated`, `result`) or at least keep the summary off stdout; change the test in the same commit and grep other `mcp` tests for "Diagnostic MCP" text assertions.
- Failing test: change `Mcp_invoke_posts_inline_json_to_external_diagnostic_route` to `Assert.DoesNotContain("12ms", result.Output)`, `Assert.Contains("12ms", result.Error)` and `JsonDocument.Parse(result.Output)` over the whole stdout (red today).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Command.Reference.md:897` (say where the summary goes)
- Design conflict: none

### R-075 · S25-01 · Build/CI · The native-runtime validator documented as the Grimoire's pre-open proof (including the shipped-binary hash check) is never called in production
- Verification: Narrowed (Blocking → Important) — V14: mechanism confirmed; the remediation is a docs correction, not wiring. Under the brief's literal scale (documented guarantee false in production) this is Blocking; rated Important because the headline guarantee (hermetic, no ambient fallback, codec present) is enforced at build/CI/test time, `FreezeProvider` and the non-empty `cipher_version` check, and macOS library validation (empty entitlements) blocks a swapped dylib; the caller may re-promote
- Category: docs-drift
- Location: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:508`; `Infra/Data/SqliteNativeRuntimeValidator.cs:19,46,77,263`; `Infra/Data/SqliteNativeRuntimeManifest.cs:147`; `src/RetroDownfall.Arcanum.NativeSqlCipher/buildTransitive/RetroDownfall.Arcanum.NativeSqlCipher.targets:29-41,62-86`; `tests/NativeSqlCipher/SqlCipherCompatibilityTests.cs:194` (only caller); `scripts/verify-native-sqlcipher.sh:101-130,457,765-767`; `scripts/packaging/macos/common.sh:178-191,297-338`; `Infra/Data/Covenant/CovenantSqliteConnectionInitializer.cs:271-300`
- Defect: DESIGN §5.4 says `ValidateAsync` "proves behavior before the Grimoire opens ... and hashes the delivered binary against the manifest. Any mismatch returns a closed error code and leaves the Grimoire unavailable"; nothing in `src/` or `scripts/` calls it (no DI registration), so the behavioral probes and the hash check never run on a user machine, and the build targets embed the whole manifest for it. Wiring it as written would break macOS: `sign_publish_dir` re-signs every nested Mach-O (`codesign --force`), so the shipped dylib's SHA-256 can never equal the manifest's ad-hoc-signed hash, producing `Grimoire.NativeRuntimeAssetHashMismatch` on every notarized install (latent Windows analogue if Authenticode signing is enabled).
- Fix: Correct the docs: rewrite DESIGN §5.4 "Runtime proof" (:508) to say the behavioral and hash proofs are build, CI and test-time gates (`verify-native-sqlcipher.sh`, `NativeSqlCipherDeliveryTests`, `SqlCipherCompatibilityTests`) and state the runtime guarantee as what actually runs (`FreezeProvider`, `cipher_version` non-empty check, macOS library validation). Fix the targets comment at `:29-33` (the embedded manifest is test-only; keep with an honest comment or remove the `EmbeddedResource` plumbing). Do not delete the validator file without updating the inventories that enumerate it by name (`GrimoireConnectionAcquisitionInventory.cs:3081-3162`, `GrimoireWalCheckpointTests.cs:99`, `SqliteConnectionInitializationInventoryTests.cs`, `CovenantConnectionDrainTests.cs:491`). If a runtime proof is still wanted it is a separate design decision (probes only, or a signature-stripped hash recorded at packaging).
- Failing test: a doc-contradiction test in the style of `DocumentationCodeContradictionTests`: if `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` contains `SqliteNativeRuntimeValidator.ValidateAsync`, then `src/` outside the validator and result files must contain a call site (fails today; green after the docs correction or a deliberate wiring). Do not write `Bootstrap_refuses_to_open_when_the_delivered_native_library_hash_differs_from_the_manifest` unless wiring is chosen, because it would pin the macOS re-sign hash hazard.
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §5.4 "Runtime proof" (:508) and the NativeSqlCipher targets comment
- Design conflict: yes — `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md:508` currently asserts the runtime validator/hash proof; per V14 (and the controller note) the remediation is to correct the DESIGN text, not to wire the validator

### R-076 · S25-02 · Build/CI · A release dispatch can overwrite the assets of an already-published release, and the auto-numbered version races between concurrent dispatches
- Verification: Confirmed (the race half Narrowed) — V14: no `concurrency:` group exists in `release.yml` (the "deliberately absent" statement is in `ci.yml:18-23` about `cancel-in-progress`; a queue-without-cancel group is consistent with it); the published-release clobber is the substantive half
- Category: reliability
- Location: `.github/workflows/release.yml:139-161,150-155,333-335`; `.github/workflows/release-macos-arm64.yml:315-317`
- Defect: an explicit `version` input (or a declared non-channel `<Version>`) is used as written with no check that `v<version>` does not already exist; `gh release view "$TAG"` succeeds for any existing release, draft or published, and `gh release upload ... --clobber` then replaces the shipped binaries and `SHA256SUMS.txt` in place, making notarized public artifacts mutable. Two simultaneous bare-channel dispatches both compute `highest+1` and interleave per-file `--clobber` uploads (zip and checksum from different runs), a double manual dispatch within a build window.
- Fix: In `prepare` after the version is validated run `gh release view "$TAG" --json isDraft --jq .isDraft` and fail unless the release is absent or a draft (same in `release-macos-arm64.yml`); add `concurrency: { group: release-${{ inputs.version || 'auto' }}, cancel-in-progress: false }` on the top-level `release.yml` (it is also `workflow_call`; never on the called jobs, never `cancel-in-progress: true`).
- Failing test: `ReleasePipelineTests.Release_refuses_to_clobber_a_published_release` (parse `release.yml`; assert a step before "Create or update the draft GitHub Release" contains `isDraft` and a top-level `concurrency:` with `cancel-in-progress: false`; red today).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.Engineering.md` release section (~778) and DESIGN §19 release notes (~4540)
- Design conflict: none

### R-077 · S25-08 · Build/CI · The Windows lane's exact-name test filters rot silently; the workflow test pins the filter string, not that the tests exist
- Verification: Confirmed — V14 (mutation applied to the real test code)
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `.github/workflows/ci.yml:441,591`; `tests/Packaging/ContinuousIntegrationWorkflowTests.cs:158-209` (`methods` array :176-193, 16 entries; `Assert.Contains(... expected ...)` :200); target e.g. `tests/GrimoireTransitions/GrimoireOfflineTransitionJournalFileStoreTests.cs:1945`; `tests/Platform/WindowsCiSurfaceTests.cs`
- Defect: 16 Windows-only methods are selected by exact `FullyQualifiedName=`; a `dotnet test` filter alternative that matches nothing is not an error. Renaming `Windows_delete_durably_succeeds_after_publication` in the test source leaves the workflow test green (it compares the workflow text to a copy of itself) while the Windows lane silently runs 15 methods; `ARCANUM_REQUIRE_WINDOWS_SUITE` only catches a lane where everything skips. Windows ACL/reparse-point/hard-link security tests can drop out of the only Windows evidence with no signal.
- Fix: Add a macOS-runnable reflection test that parses the `FullyQualifiedName=` entries from both `ci.yml` filters (and the Compendium filter at `ContinuousIntegrationWorkflowTests.cs:205`), loads the test assembly and asserts each resolves to exactly one method carrying `[Fact]`/`[SkippableFact]`/`[Theory]`.
- Failing test: `ContinuousIntegrationWorkflowTests.Every_exact_windows_filter_names_an_existing_test_method` (red after any rename; green once workflow and test move together).
- Docs: none
- Design conflict: none

### R-078 · S21-01 / S21-02 / S28-01 / S28-02 / V-S21-01 / V-S28-01 · Tests · Three test classes set `ARCANUM_TEST_HOME` without the Testing environment and write the developer's real `~/.config/arcanum`; the CLI harness gives production DI the real profile; a profile-prefix guard would false-positive on Windows
- Verification: Confirmed (S28-01 Blocking → Important; S21-02/S28-02 Narrowed by V12 then re-affirmed by V19: the real write is proven by the test's own `Assert.Equal(3, handler.Requests.Count)`) — V12, V19; V12's "GlobalConsole races" hazard was judged wrong by V19 (`GlobalConsoleCollection` is `DisableParallelization`)
- Category: test-honesty
- Location: `tests/Cli/ConfigurationCommandServiceTests.cs:34-48,65-69,101-105,127,158-162`; `tests/Configuration/ConfigurationBootstrapperTests.cs:20-47,376-379,415,461`; `tests/InstallationReset/StoppedHostGrimoireConnectionAuthorityTests.cs:35-42,86,191` (the third, unlisted escape: creates the real `GrimoireDirectory` and takes its maintenance lock); `tests/Cli/CliTestHarness.cs:54-110,112-130`; `tests/Cli/WorkspaceCommandTests.cs:142-167,688-713`; `Cli/UX/RecentResourceStore.cs:20-24,48-141`; `Cli/Infrastructure/CliApplicationFactory.cs:130`; `Infra/DependencyInjection/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:159-164`; gate `Core/Storage/ArcanumPaths.cs:7-16,41-53,134-160`; `tests/Support/TestProcessPaths.cs:7-14`; helper to lift `tests/RetroDownfall.Compendium.Tests/ArcanumTestHomeScope.cs`; contract `tests/Collections/EnvironmentIsolationContractTests.cs`
- Defect: `ArcanumPaths.TestingHome` returns null unless `DOTNET_ENVIRONMENT` or `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT` equals `Testing`; the three classes set only `ARCANUM_TEST_HOME`, so they overwrite the real `arcanum.json` with a stub (and `ConfigurationWriter` rewrites it) and take a maintenance lock in the real directory; nothing restores the bytes, contradicting DESIGN :3803. The sibling list in S21-01 was wrong: `ArcanumWebApplicationFactoryTests` (the factory sets Testing in its constructor) and `GrimoireAdmissionBenchmarkPackagingTests` (only strips the variable from child environments) are NOT escapes. Separately 61 of the 68 `CliTestHarness`/`ConfigureCliServices` files never redirect the home, so every such run reads the developer's real `arcanum.json`, and name-resolved selections (`workspace show demo`, `mcp show`, `session show "Archived quest"`) reach `RecentResourceStore.RememberUnderExclusive`, which creates the real directory and `recent-resources.txt` through the real mutation boundary. Not observed on disk (the developer's `arcanum.json` is a 6.6 KB real file); the escape is unconditional whenever the ambient environment is not `Testing`, the default for `dotnet test`. CI runners are disposable so it is invisible there.
- Fix: Reconciled fix (V19): (1) lift the Compendium `ArcanumTestHomeScope` (sets HOME, USERPROFILE, both host environments and `ARCANUM_TEST_HOME`, restores all) into `tests/Support/` and use it in the three escaping classes; delete the four private `TestingEnvironmentScope` copies and `AttachmentCommandTests.TestHomeScope` later, not in this change. (2) Add a `CliTestHarness.RunAsync` precondition: if `ArcanumPaths.GrimoireDirectory`/`SecretStoreDirectory` resolves to the real `<OriginalUserProfile>/.config/arcanum` (or real `ApplicationData/arcanum`) open a harness-owned scope for the run, and leave it alone when the ambient home is already redirected (six classes redirect themselves). (3) Reject fail-closed `ArcanumPaths` hardening: `ArcanumPathsTestingOverrideTests.Persistent_paths_ignore_test_home_outside_testing_environment` pins the deliberate ignore and a throw would turn a stray variable into a production start failure. (4) Defer the module-initializer process default and the XunitTestFramework tamper check (see R-391). Guard predicate hazard (V-S28-01): compare against the EXACT real directories (`<profile>/.config/arcanum`, `<ApplicationData>/arcanum`) with full-path comparison and a trailing-separator check; a profile-prefix predicate false-positives on Windows because `Path.GetTempPath()` lives under `%USERPROFILE%`.
- Failing test: `EnvironmentIsolationContractTests.Every_test_class_that_sets_ARCANUM_TEST_HOME_also_sets_a_Testing_environment` (extend the IL walker to resolve `OperandType.InlineString` through `module.ResolveString`; accept either the shared scope type or both `ldstr`s in the same outermost class; red for exactly the three classes) plus `CliHarnessIsolationTests.Name_resolved_resource_selection_stays_inside_the_test_home` (reuse the `workspace show demo` scenario; the `RecordingHandler` records `ArcanumPaths.GrimoireDirectory`/`SecretStoreDirectory` mid-command; assert neither equals or lies under the exact real directories) and `TestHomeGuardTests.Guard_accepts_a_temp_root_under_the_profile_and_rejects_the_real_config_directory`. Prefer the mid-command capture over a before/after mtime snapshot of the real directory (which false-fails when the operator's own host touches it).
- Docs: `docs/Arcanum.DESIGN.md` §13 (:3803): name the mechanical enforcement
- Design conflict: none

Verification: Narrowed (Important → Minor), V3: converges by idempotence (`CompareRemove` treats already-absent as done).

Verification: Narrowed (Important → Minor), V3: V2/V3 records are AEAD-sealed and cannot carry retained accounts; only a legacy V1 file is untrusted. DESIGN §10.19.6/§11.2.1.

Design conflict: partial — DESIGN §11.2 item 4 documents the permissive Get; the fix must write the chosen policy there.

## Refuted (dropped)
- S17-06 · "Deterministic no-progress" can be evaded by volatile tool results or cycles longer than 8 rounds · V18 · Refuted: the behavior is the documented contract (DESIGN :56 "no fixed ... ceiling ... stops on deterministic repeated no-progress", :1657 "one of the last 8 rounds", CHAT-LOOP :38 "exact recurrence is no progress", Engineering :25/:65 operator cancel); trimming still ends in `Hub.ContextBudgetExceeded`; the real backstop gap is R-053.
- S17-07 · Spend is recorded as zero whenever the provider reports no usage · V11 · Refuted: DESIGN :5025 (§22.2 "Missing usage") states a no-usage call contributes no `BillableOperations` row and that Arcanum "does not fabricate billable token counts from the admission estimate"; the proposed estimate fallback is the exact thing the doc forbids.
- S18-05 · `attach_session_file` reads the entire attachment into memory before applying the size caps · V8 · Refuted: every stored Text/Image row was size-validated at ingest (`SessionAttachmentContentPolicy.Validate`, text capped at the 1 MiB `CliMaxAttachFileSizeBytes`, images at `ScryingMaxImageBytes`, default 1 MiB, clamp 20 MiB; inline turn attachments validated in `PingRequestPreflightValidator`), so `record.ByteLength` is bounded; the 256 MiB figure is a per-session total, not a per-file size.
- S19-02 · A client disconnect during `/v1/chat/completions` streaming still marks the Idempotency claim terminal and replays a truncated "completed" stream · V8 · Refuted: `ct` carries the disconnect only when `continueThenReplay == false` (`OpenAiV1Endpoints.cs:462-466`); `DisconnectPolicy` is fixed at `Auto` (no setting site), under which `ResolveContinueThenReplay` is true exactly when an `Idempotency-Key` is present, so with a key the stream is deliberately drained and completed with the full replayable body (ADR 0003/0004), and without a key no claim exists.
